### USER
anda adalah asisten programer AI yang bertujuan untuk menemukan bug, meng evaluasi efisiensi script suatu program.

program yang di analisa adalah program indie hasil kerjaan AI dari copilot versi pro terbatas yang di upload di github secara public.

program ini juga saya upload di github public:
https://github.com/ImaterialC/Misora-Editor

dalam menyampaikan ke saya anda gunakan bahasa indonesia, dalam pembuatan prompt, debat dan kritik antar ai gunakan bahasa inggris, dalam dokumentasi juga gunakan bahasa inggris.

hasil jadi nya adalah berupa perintah kepada copilot untuk melakukan enginering, entah itu memperbaiki bug, menambah fitur, membuat rencana, dokumentasi, dll.

tapi sebelum membuat hasil akhir, hasil riset, brainstrom anda terhadap program ini akan di uji dahulu oleh asisten AI lain, dan kalian akan saling crosscheck dan saling kritik tentang temuan bug, fitur dan ide yang sudah ada dan yang ditemukan.


Phase -1
tanyakan dulu apakah anda bisa akses semua file atau ada file yang kurang atau tidak terbaca?
jabarkan ketersediaan akses mu dan katakan kalau kamu memiliki segala files dan item untuk masuk ke phase berikutnya.

phase 0 riset mandiri
anda meriset mandiri tentang program Misora untuk menemukan bug, optimasi, menambah fitur, atau ide terkait program Misora ini. hasil jadi nya adalah sebuah hasil riset dan temuan bug, optimasi, menambah fitur, atau ide terkait program Misora ini dan dokumentasi dalam block text yang akan dimasukan ke AI lain untuk memulai debat

phase 1 
rapat dari hasil riset mandiri bersama Ai lain dan saya 
dari sini anda akan memulai banyak sekali debat yang mungkin akan berlangsung aga lama sampai kalian semua dan saya sepakat 
jika semuanya sepakat dan saya aprove baru masuk ke phase 2.

phase 2
pembuatan prompt yang akan dimasukan ke dalam copilot
setelah sepakat kalian masing masing akan mereview kembali prompt buatan kalian.
jangan sampai ada halusinasi atau overenginering pembuatan prompt sehingga menjadi hambatan bagi kemampuan copilot itu sendiri.
saya biasanya menggunakan gemini flash 3.8 high selama ada quota, dan jika habis maka saya akan pindah ke Claude Sonnet 4.6
ini adalah prefrensi saya sebagai pemula dan keterbatasanku dalam plan dan berlangganannya akun copilot saya.

hasilnya adalah berupa text block berisi prompt copilot hasil riset dan kesepakatan di phase 2.

saya akan uji lagi hasil prompt kalian dengan masing masing ai. dan kalian harus jujur prompt siapa yang layak dimasukan ke dalam copilot.

jika kalian sepakat itu berarti isi prompt kalian akan sama persis masing masing ai.
dan akan melakukan ke phase 3

phase 3 run copilot
disini tugas kalian sebenarnya sudah selesai dan antara saya dan copilot saja
tapi mungkin akan ada terjadi eror loop yang kemungkinan meminta bantuan kalian.

Mengenai progress project ini anda bisa baca di
PROJECT_STATE.md

### ASSISTANT
# Phase 0 — Riset Mandiri Selesai ✅

Saya sudah melakukan riset mendalam dengan **verifikasi byte-exact**, bukan sekadar membaca paste chat. Berikut ringkasannya untuk Anda, lalu dokumen debat (bahasa Inggris) di bawah.

## Cara saya memverifikasi (penting untuk kredibilitas)
- Sandbox saya ternyata bisa mengakses `api.github.com` → saya unduh **tarball repo utuh** (73 file, `misora.py` = 499.293 bytes, cocok dengan tree). Ini menggantikan "disk" sesuai izin Anda, dan **bebas dari distorsi escape** yang merusak paste chat.
- `compileall` **PASS** (Python 3.10 sandbox; target Anda 3.12 — saya catat sebagai caveat).
- Saya **menjalankan repro runtime nyata** untuk modul core murni-Python (undo_journal, story_ast, script_codec, ngui_tags, search_engine, xunity_sync) — bukan mengarang hasil.
- ⚠️ Saya **TIDAK** menjalankan suite resmi 100 test (butuh display Tk + UnityPy + fixture di mesin Windows Anda). Itu tetap tugas Anda (Rule 2/29).

## Temuan utama yang HARUS diperbaiki (terverifikasi)

**🔴 Integritas data / kehilangan data (P0–P1):**
1. **Copy Box → Paste Box mem-flatten segmen & membuang voice cue** (`on_box_paste` mengubah snapshot multi-segmen jadi 1 teks). Round-trip tidak zero-drift.
2. **XUAT sync berhenti diam-diam setelah save pertama** — baseline JP ditimpa teks Inggris + pakai indeks posisi. Saya buktikan dengan repro: sync ke-2 = 0 match. Core sudah punya registry JP per-uid tapi GUI tidak memakainya.
3. **Close File tetap menutup meski save gagal** → edit hilang.
4. **Exit hanya menyimpan file aktif** → file dirty lain dibuang.
5. **Save plain-text saat Preview Compiled ON** menulis teks rendered (markup NGUI rusak).
6. **Replace All multi-file** menimpa edit unsaved file aktif + melewati Translation Lock + decode `errors="replace"` merusak dictionary.
7. **Batch Translate berjalan di thread worker** mengubah dokumen/undo/UI langsung (race) + `end_batch` tanpa `finally`.

**🟠 Fungsional/UI (P1–P2):**
8. **Ctrl+Alt+X/C/V/A mati** — tooltip mengiklankan tapi tidak ada binding; router memblokir semua Ctrl+Alt. Ledger klaim RESOLVED, **kode membantah**.
9. **Ctrl+T konflik** — di editor malah Copy AI Prompt, padahal menu = Translator Assistant.
10. **Speaker edit melewati central commit** (false-dirty, tanpa uid/strip).
11. **Pindah file antar-tab** membocorkan state lock/modified file sebelumnya.
12. **Dirty state global, bukan per-file** (repro: edit B membuat A "dirty", save B membuat A "clean").
13. **Eyedropper mengubah DPI awareness runtime** (melanggar Rule 23).

**🟡 Kosmetik/dokumen (P3):** versi picker hardcode "v1.3.3"; label tombol gradient pakai koma padahal insert pakai hyphen.

## Koreksi penting (supaya tidak buang waktu) 🔁
- **SPAN-NFKC-PER-CHAR**: TIDAK ter-reproduksi (tokenizer sudah menggabung combining mark; `が` cocok). Usul **RETRACT**.
- **STALE-CARD-REGISTRY**: sudah diperbaiki (`clear()` sebelum return). Usul **RESOLVED**.
- Glossary line-break, `mark_saved/is_clean`, empty-arg `""→" "`, uid serialization: **bukan bug** (sudah saya verifikasi; sebagian artefak transport).

## Temuan proses paling penting
**LEDGER-DRIFT**: beberapa klaim "RESOLVED" di `PROJECT_STATE.md` **bertentangan dengan kode di `main`** (Ctrl+Alt, destroy sweep, after_idle deferral, versi picker). Ledger kanonik terlalu optimis. Phase 1 wajib rekonsiliasi ledger-vs-kode sebelum kerja baru.

## 📋 Dokumen debat untuk AI lawan (bahasa Inggris)

Salin blok di bawah ini ke Copilot (atau AI lawan) untuk memulai Phase 1:

```text
# MISORA EDITOR — PHASE 0 INDEPENDENT RESEARCH BRIEF (FOR CROSS-AI DEBATE)

Author: Research Assistant A (static + runtime audit)
Target repo: github.com/ImaterialC/Misora-Editor, branch main, commit 49cb3d1 (2026-11-21)
Source of truth: byte-exact repository tarball (NOT chat paste). misora.py = 499,293 bytes / 11,169 lines.
Your role: Critically cross-check every claim below. Agree only with evidence; challenge with file:line or a minimal repro. Do not invent fixtures or platform behavior.

## 0. GROUND RULES (from AGENTS.md — binding)
- Classify each item: CONFIRMED / CONDITIONAL / DESIGN INVARIANT / RETRACTED.
- Use stable slugs (no new B-numbers). The canonical ledger is PROJECT_STATE.md, but it is a CLAIM to verify, not authority.
- Respect invariants: one Tk root; Seg.record direct reference; Box.clean_text/.text/.raw read-only; BoxSnapshot preserves seg_texts; central edit commit; first-position whitelist shortcut router; workers never touch Tk/doc/journal; revision-based dirty; immutable JP keys per Box uid; atomic bundle save; TLS verification on; NGUI single source in core/ngui_tags.py; CSIF headers never inside Box.clean_text.
- Scope discipline (Rule 24): no premature CSIF/MTL/PromptStudio/LRU/splash redesign. Minimal diffs.

## 1. VERIFICATION ENVIRONMENT & LIMITATIONS (honest)
- Sandbox: Python 3.10.9 / Linux. `compileall misora.py core assets tests` => PASS (exit 0). Caveat: user target is Python 3.12 on Windows.
- RUNTIME repros executed for pure-Python core modules (undo_journal, story_ast, script_codec, ngui_tags, search_engine, xunity_sync). Evidence tagged [REPRO] below is real executed output.
- NOT run: the official 100-test unittest suite (requires Tk display + UnityPy + storydata_1001005.unity3d fixture on the user's Windows machine). Manual Windows tests (Rule 29) remain the user's responsibility.
- Transport note: chat-pasted code and naive web extraction MANGLE backslash escapes and indentation. All findings below were verified against the byte-exact tarball. Do not reason from paste.

## 2. META-FINDING — LEDGER-DRIFT (highest process priority)
Several PROJECT_STATE.md "RESOLVED" claims are CONTRADICTED by current main. The ledger overstates completion. Phase 1 MUST reconcile ledger-vs-code before new feature work. Specific contradictions are items N1, N3, N4, N17 below. Proposed action: a ledger-reconciliation pass that re-tests each RESOLVED slug against disk and downgrades false ones.

## 3. NEW CONFIRMED FINDINGS (not in ledger, or ledger falsely resolved)

N1. CTRL-ALT-HOTKEYS-BLOCKED — REOPEN (ledger says RESOLVED; code contradicts)
  Severity: P1. Evidence [CODE]: misora.py:5764 is_alt = (state & 0x0008) or (state & 0x0020); :5767-5768 `if is_ctrl and is_alt: return None` (blocks ALL Ctrl+Alt). NO 0x20000/MOD2_MASK anywhere; NO <Control-Alt-*> bindings anywhere. Yet tooltips advertise Ctrl+Alt+A/X/C/V (misora.py:3686,3729,3737,3745).
  Impact: advertised box cut/copy/paste/select accelerators are dead; Rule 12 ("every advertised accelerator must have a real binding") violated. Ledger's claimed MOD2_MASK fix + whitelist + root bindings are absent.
  Minimal fix direction: add the documented Ctrl+Alt whitelist branch (distinguish real Alt from AltGr via 0x20000) OR add explicit root <Control-Alt-x/c/v/a> bindings; align router. Add a binding-presence test.
  Question: Was this fix lost in the stash-pop merge that produced the conflict markers, or never applied? Verify against local D:\Misora-Editor.

N2. SHORTCUT-CTRL-T-CONFLICT — NEW
  Severity: P2. Evidence [CODE]: router misora.py:5808-5810 plain Ctrl+T -> on_copy_ai_prompt(); root :6043 <Control-t> -> on_open_translator_assistant(); menu advertises "Translator Assistant... Ctrl+T".
  Impact: inside editor widgets (MisoraShortcuts bindtag first) Ctrl+T performs the WRONG action vs menu/root. Inconsistent accelerator.
  Minimal fix: router plain Ctrl+T should open Translator Assistant; keep Ctrl+Shift+T = Copy AI Prompt.

N3. AFTER-TEARDOWN-SWEEP-MISSING — REOPEN (ledger claims full sweep)
  Severity: P2. Evidence [CODE]: MisoraApp.destroy (misora.py:1375-1384) cancels ONLY _search_poll_job. NO cancellation of _cont_resize_job/_toast_after_id/_eyedropper_after_id; NO tk.call("after","info") sweep (grep: absent).
  Impact: "invalid command name" Tcl errors after close — the exact symptom ledger Phase 2 claims fixed.
  Minimal fix: cancel known after-jobs + anonymous sweep in destroy(), guarded.

N4. STARTUP-RESTORE-NOT-DEFERRED — REOPEN (ledger claims after_idle deferral)
  Severity: P2 (perf-claim drift). Evidence [CODE]: misora.py:1268-1289 restores initial_path/_saved_opened_files INLINE in __init__ (with splash _rpt), NOT via after_idle. Ledger Phase 3 / Rule 22 claims deferral until root displayed.
  Impact: startup blocks during session restore; the claimed deferral is not implemented as described. Measure before claiming improvement (Rule 22).
  Question: Is inline-with-splash acceptable, or must it be after_idle per ledger? Reconcile claim vs intent.

N5. BOX-PASTE-FLATTEN-SEGMENTS — NEW (data integrity)
  Severity: P0/P1. Evidence [CODE+REPRO]: on_box_paste (misora.py:6839-6881) converts internal _box_clipboard BoxSnapshots -> DialogueBlock(text=snap.clean_text) -> BoxSnapshot(seg_texts=[b.text], voice_cue=""). [REPRO] BoxSnapshot.from_box preserves seg_texts=['NEW','']; the GUI round-trip collapses to 1 seg and drops voice_cue.
  Impact: Copy Box -> Paste Box loses multi-segment boundaries AND voice cues (game audio desync). Violates Rule 5 (preserve seg_texts) and Rule 26/29 zero-drift round-trip. Note: external CSIF paste inherently flattens (format limitation, see N5b), but the INTERNAL clipboard could insert snapshots directly.
  Minimal fix: when _box_clipboard is valid, insert the original BoxSnapshot objects directly (preserve seg_texts/voice_cue/extra_args); only flatten for external CSIF.
  N5b [REPRO]: serialize->parse merges a 2-seg box into one block 's1s2'. CSIF is per-box; multi-seg loss is inherent to the interchange format. Debate: document this limitation explicitly; do not claim CSIF round-trip is seg-lossless.

N6. SPEAKER-EDIT-BYPASSES-COMMIT — NEW
  Severity: P1/P2. Evidence [CODE]: entry_speaker.bind("<KeyRelease>", -> _on_speaker_edited) (misora.py:3526) bypasses _commit_widget_edit. _on_speaker_edited uses speaker_var.get() (NO .strip()), records BoxEditDelta WITHOUT box_uid, sets is_dirty=True unconditionally (even arrow keys), and does NOT call _dirty_state_changed. The central path's speaker branch DOES strip + pass box_uid.
  Impact: Rule 11 central-commit violation; false-dirty on non-edit keystrokes; inconsistent speaker normalization and undo identity between two paths.
  Minimal fix: route entry_speaker through _commit_widget_edit; remove the duplicate direct binding; only mark dirty on actual change.

N7. DOC-SWITCH-STATE-BLEED — NEW
  Severity: P1. Evidence [CODE]: open_file cache-hit branch (misora.py:6083-6099) does NOT clear/restore _locked_boxes or _modified_boxes, resets current_box_idx=0, and rebuilds _original_box_texts from CURRENT doc text. Fresh-open path (:6105-6107) clears them.
  Impact: switching tabs leaks file A's lock/modified indices into file B; per-document view state/selection not saved/restored (Rule 16). Wrong boxes may be locked/synced.
  Minimal fix: store per-document state (locks, modified, box_idx, view, JP snapshots) in open_documents entry; save on switch-away, restore on switch-in.

N8. XUAT-JP-KEY-DRIFT — NEW (data integrity)
  Severity: P1. Evidence [CODE+REPRO]: on_save (misora.py:7933,7943) uses positional _original_box_texts as japanese_key, then OVERWRITES it with box_obj.clean_text (English). [REPRO] XUnitySyncManager.find_and_update: 1st call with JP key -> 1 match (dict updated); 2nd call with drifted EN key -> 0 matches. Core ALREADY provides register_jp_key(box_uid)/get_jp_key(box_uid) (xunity_sync.py:79,84) but misora.py NEVER calls them (grep: absent).
  Impact: after first save, XUAT sync silently stops; positional indices shift after structural edits -> wrong JP keys paired with wrong boxes (Rule 10: immutable JP keys per uid).
  Minimal fix: wire GUI to sync_manager.register_jp_key on load (uid-keyed, immutable) and get_jp_key on save; stop overwriting baseline with English.

N9. CLOSE-FILE-IGNORES-SAVE-FAILURE — NEW (data loss)
  Severity: P1. Evidence [CODE]: close_file (misora.py:9037-9046) calls on_save() then UNCONDITIONALLY deletes open_documents entry + prunes undo. No success check. (App-exit path :1357 DOES check is_dirty.)
  Impact: user chooses "Save", save fails (disk/verify error), file still closed and edits discarded. Rule 16: "Failed save aborts close."
  Minimal fix: after on_save(), if still dirty -> abort close.

N10. EXIT-SAVES-ONLY-ACTIVE — NEW (data loss)
  Severity: P1. Evidence [CODE]: _on_app_exit (misora.py:1351-1358) prompts if ANY doc dirty but on_save() saves only the ACTIVE file; checks only active is_dirty.
  Impact: other dirty open documents are silently discarded on exit.
  Minimal fix: iterate dirty docs (save each, abort exit on any failure) or prompt per-file.

N11. PLAINTEXT-SAVE-COMPILED-CORRUPTION — NEW (data loss)
  Severity: P1. Evidence [CODE]: _update_views plain-text branch (misora.py:6190-6203) inserts COMPILED rendered text + disables widget when Preview Compiled ON. on_save plain branch (:7869) reads widget content directly -> writes rendered text.
  Impact: saving a plain .txt while compiled preview is active strips/transforms NGUI markup permanently (backup mitigates). Bundle/v2 paths are safe (use model).
  Minimal fix: plain-text save must use _plain_text_content (model), never the compiled widget; or force raw mode before save.

N12. BULK-REPLACE-CLOBBERS-DIRTY-CURRENT — NEW (data loss + lock bypass)
  Severity: P1. Evidence [CODE]: on_replace_all multi-file loop (misora.py:8762-8798): bundle branch has NO is_box_locked check and NO per_file_deltas; :8797-8798 reloads current file from disk (reload_from_disk=True) discarding unsaved in-memory edits.
  Impact: unsaved edits of the active file lost; Translation Lock bypassed for the active file; bundle replacements not undoable.
  Minimal fix: flush/merge active doc edits before bulk op; honor active-file locks; journal bundle deltas; avoid clobbering dirty current doc.

N13. REPLACE-ALL-TEXT-DESTRUCTIVE-DECODE — NEW
  Severity: P1/P2. Evidence [CODE]: misora.py:8776 reads .txt with errors="replace" then rewrites whole file (:8783). Also open_file plain read :6138 errors="replace".
  Impact: non-UTF8 bytes permanently replaced (U+FFFD) on rewrite — violates Rule 10 strict decode for dictionaries (backup mitigates).
  Minimal fix: strict decode; on failure skip+report rather than lossy rewrite; preserve BOM/EOL.

N14. BATCH-TRANSLATE-THREAD-UNSAFE — NEW
  Severity: P1. Evidence [CODE]: BatchTranslateDialog.worker (misora.py:11089-11145) runs in threading.Thread and calls undo_journal.begin_batch/record/end_batch, doc.inject_box, _modified_boxes.add, and self.after_idle(...) — all from the worker thread. end_batch (:11128) NOT in finally; box=doc.boxes[b_idx] (:11096) captured pre-translate with no uid guard.
  Impact: Rule 15 violation (workers mutate doc/journal + call Tk); race with UI; if an exception escapes the per-box try, journal stuck batching -> undo dead; structural edits during batch mis-target boxes.
  Minimal fix: worker computes translations only; post results via main-thread queue; apply doc/journal mutations on UI thread; begin_batch/end_batch in finally; validate box uid before apply.

N15. MTL-CALLBACK-TK-FROM-WORKER — NEW
  Severity: P2. Evidence [CODE]: translate_async (mtl_engine.py:345-351) invokes on_success/on_error IN the worker thread; misora.py callbacks call self.after_idle(...) from that thread (e.g., test connection :10512). Tk is not thread-safe.
  Impact: Rule 15 violation; potential crash/corruption on some Tk builds.
  Minimal fix: marshal callbacks through the existing main-thread queue pattern (as search does), not after_idle from workers.

N16. DIRTY-STATE-GLOBAL-NOT-PER-FILE — NEW
  Severity: P2. Evidence [REPRO]: MasterUndoJournal.mark_saved/is_clean accept file_path but IGNORE it (global _revision/_saved_revision). Repro: edit B -> is_clean(A)=False (A falsely dirty); save B -> is_clean(A)=True (A falsely clean). prune_file removes ops but does not reconcile _revision/_saved_revision.
  Impact: with multiple files open, dirty indicators and undo-to-clean are wrong across files (Rule 13 intent is per-document).
  Minimal fix: per-file revision tracking (key by path) or per-document journals; reconcile counters on prune.

N17. VERSION-HARDCODED-PICKER — NEW (Rule 21 drift)
  Severity: P3. Evidence [CODE]: NguiColorPickerPopup title "NGUI Color & Gradient Picker (v1.3.3)" (misora.py:488) hardcoded; ledger claims version unified dynamically incl. picker.
  Minimal fix: use __version__/_get_app_version().

N18. GRADIENT-LABEL-COMMA — NEW (cosmetic)
  Severity: P3. Evidence [CODE]: button label f"Insert [{c0_code},{c1_code}]" (misora.py:907) shows COMMA, but _commit_gradient_color inserts HYPHEN [c0-c1]. Misleading UI (Rule 19: comma accepted on input, normalized; display should show hyphen).
  Minimal fix: label uses hyphen.

N19. EYEDROPPER-DPI-RUNTIME — NEW (Rule 23 violation)
  Severity: P2. Evidence [CODE]: ctypes.windll.shcore.SetProcessDpiAwareness(1) in _toggle_eyedropper (misora.py:957). Rule 23: do not change process DPI awareness at runtime.
  Impact: can rescale all windows / break coordinate mapping mid-session.
  Minimal fix: remove runtime DPI change; rely on process manifest / existing awareness; sample coordinates accordingly.

N20. UNDO-COALESCE-CROSS-SOURCE — REFINE ledger (core fixed, GUI untagged)
  Severity: P2. Evidence [CODE+REPRO]: undo_journal.record compares delta.source (default "keyboard"); BoxEditDelta.source default="keyboard". misora.py passes source= ONLY at paste_caret (:6954,6976). [REPRO] ribbon-cut delta + typing delta (both default source) coalesce into 1 op (coalesced=True).
  Impact: ledger claims source tracking added, but because GUI callers don't tag source, ribbon cuts still merge with typing. Effectively OPEN at GUI layer.
  Minimal fix: tag source at call sites (ribbon/mtl/replace/speaker) so coalescing respects boundaries.

## 4. LEDGER-OPEN ITEMS RE-VERIFIED (still accurate)
- REPLACE-ALL-BUNDLE-PREFILTER: OPEN [CODE] raw.count(find_text.encode) on bundle bytes (misora.py:8725-8726).
- REPLACE-ALL-UNDO-GAP: OPEN [CODE] bundle branch records no per_file_deltas (:8764-8774).
- SEARCH-RACE: OPEN [CODE] on_start_search passes LIVE doc refs mem_docs[fp]=entry["doc"] (:8032) to worker. Phase 2 roadmap checkbox "[x] immutable snapshots" is FALSE.
- FOLDER-DROP-EXTRA-IGNORED: OPEN [CODE] dirs[0] only (:2287).
- BULK-DELTA-NONATOMIC: ledger-CONFIRMED; consistent with non-atomic text writes in on_replace_all. (Revert path _apply_delta BulkFileDelta fp.write_text — confirm exact line in Phase 1.)

## 5. RETRACTIONS / CORRECTIONS (do NOT spend time here)
- SPAN-NFKC-PER-CHAR: RETRACT as current bug. [REPRO] _TOKEN_RE groups base+combining marks (.[\u0300-\u036f\u3099\u309a]*); per-token NFKC composes か+゙ -> が; matcher returns True. Whole-string norm_search agrees. (Minor sub-token span granularity exists for ligature/casefold expansion, but not the claimed dakuten failure.)
- STALE-CARD-REGISTRY: RESOLVED. [CODE] _card_widgets.clear() at :7049 BEFORE plain-text early return :7051. (Residual: old card widgets not destroyed in plain-text path linger hidden until next story render — minor, bounded.)
- Glossary "Explicit Line Break": CORRECT. Source is "tag":"\\n" (literal backslash-n token). Earlier "n" appearance was a transport artifact. Not a bug.
- mark_saved/is_clean "TypeError -> no-arg fix": journal signatures are mark_saved(file_path=None)/is_clean(file_path=None); calls with args are VALID (param ignored). No TypeError. Ledger wording misleading; real issue is N16 (global state).
- Empty binary arg ""->" ": INTENTIONAL. to_binary (:723-725) documents length-0 terminates arg list; single space preserves arity. Not a bug (format constraint).
- Record.uid serialization: NOT serialized. uid is itertools.count(1) small ints; to_v2_text renders cmd/args only. Earlier "uid in v2=True" was small-int substring coincidence. Rule 4 satisfied.
- TLS-VERIFICATION-DISABLED: RESOLVED confirmed. ssl.create_default_context() used for all urlopen (mtl_engine.py:111,182,...).
- INJECT-EXTRA-ARGS / NARRATION-EMPTY-PHANTOM / inject ownership: RESOLVED confirmed via [REPRO] (extra arg preserved; print->tag on empty speaker; foreign record rejected).

## 6. EFFICIENCY OBSERVATIONS (no premature refactor — Rule 24)
- misora.py is an 11,169-line monolith; maintainability risk. Note only; do not split prematurely.
- safe_clipboard_get runs on EVERY Ctrl+V in router (up to 5x50ms retry = 250ms UI stall if clipboard locked). Consider caching/async.
- _render_continuous_view rebuilds ALL cards on navigation/lock toggle (O(N) widget churn). Card virtualization remains a valid P2 (THEME-RAW-PERF).
- Search has no snapshot/cache (SEARCH-CACHE design) and passes live docs (race, see SEARCH-RACE).

## 7. FEATURE / WORKFLOW IDEAS (deferred; for debate, low priority)

- Refresh README Project Structure (stale: omits docs/, new core/tests, Splash_4..8; references nonexistent banner.jpg/misora_chibi.png).
```

### USER
hasilnya ada di sini 
https://github.com/ImaterialC/Reference-Files

katakan bahwa kamu bisa meng akses seluruh filenya
jangan mulai debat jika kamu tidak bisa akses file atau rusak.

juga saya punya ide,
Goal utama saya adalah membuat terjemahan fans pada game princess connect redive
untuk saat ini terjemahan menggunakan model scan and replace dari plugin XUnityAutoTranslate dalam framework Bepinex.
expresinya adalah 
JP=EN
tapi kendala terjadi karena replace ini berseifat global, artinya terjemahan rekaman line yang sama akan mewakili untuk seluruh game
seiring waktu total line dalam seluruh proyek mencapai lebih dari 300ribu baris.
ini membuat load semakin berat 
dan juga kendala lain adalah saat menerjemahkan, XUAT tidak peduli dengan urutan dia cuma menampilkan rekaman, dan jika itu di masukan ke mtl atau ai kebanyakan akan sulit mengetahui konteks karena urutan line bisa acak acakan.

saya ingin pelan pelan melakukan migrasi menggunakan redirect aset
dimana itu akan mengubah segalanya.

saya sudah membuat program berbasis pyton dimana file aset story unity3d
akan dicocokan dengan seluruh folder kerja dan terjemahan xuat.

beberapa fiturnya adalah:

-scan otomatis dari awal sampai akir dan auto export menjadi file raw unity3d

-terkadang ada line yang sama tapi terjemahan berbeda tersebar di sepanjang folder project, user bisa memilih terjemahan mana yang mau dipakai.


nah itu ingin saya implementasikan di Misora.
tapi dalam UImisora pengeditan dilakukan tiap 1 box, dan itu kemungkinan adalah 1 line pada xyntax XUAT.

kalau tidak salah Misora yang sekarang memang bisa membaca xuat dan menscan project, tapi itu 1 per 1.
atau ditampilkan dalam salah satu drawer mtl.

konsep imajinasi saya adalah ada tombol atau di menu utama atau menu file yaitu import from XUAT
ketika di eksekusi akan muncul windows baru, dimana tampilan mirip dengan continous view di sebelah kiri, dan sebelah kanan adalah hasil scan dari project.
jika ditemukan terjemahan duplikat dalam proyek. maka akan ada tombol kanan dan kiri di tiap box. ada juga tombol lain yaitu mtl jika misal tidak puas dengan terjemahan xuat yang ada dan mau bandingkan dengan mtl.
(mungkin ada pengaturan dimana jika diaktifkan alih alih ada 2 continous display menjadi 3 dimana yang ke tiga adalah mtl)

itu masih konsep kritik dan perubahan sangat bebas dan dianjurkan, atau mungkin punya ide lain yang lebih baik yang merombak ide saya, silahkan.

berikut adalah hasil debat para ai saya lampirkan juga dokumen pendukung tentang project lama saya yang ingin saya integrasikan di misora.

# Phase 1 — Kontribusi Debat Saya

## Ringkasan cepat (Indonesia)

**Konvergensi kuat 3–4 AI** (saya + Grok + Qwen): Replace-All prefilter byte bundle, undo gap bundle, search race, bulk non-atomik, folder DnD ekstra, encoding `errors=replace`, conflict-marker ledger, STALE-CARD sudah resolved, UNDO-COALESCE masalahnya di caller bukan journal.

**Saya mengoreksi diri sendiri** 🙋: Grok benar tentang **SPAN-NFKC halfwidth** (`ｶﾞ` = U+FF9E/FF9F). Retraksi saya sebelumnya terlalu luas — tokenizer Misora memang menggabung U+3099 (kasus saya), tapi **tidak** U+FF9E/FF9F. Jadi bug ini **CONFIRMED untuk kasus halfwidth**. Ini kemenangan cross-check.

**Saya menantang Qwen** ⚔️: Qwen bilang "Phases 1–5 RESOLVED jangan di-prompt ulang (shortcut bindtag, DPAPI+TLS, version single source)". Bukti byte-exact saya membantah sebagian: **Ctrl+Alt mati total (N1), destroy sweep tidak ada (N3), after_idle deferral tidak ada (N4), versi picker hardcode (N17)**. Qwen membaca statis tapi melewatkan ini.

**Temuan baru Grok yang saya terima**: DPAPI fail-open (P1-3), search menelan error diam-diam (P2-1), distribusi test 27/8 vs ledger 26/9, 9 gambar README hilang.

**Ide XUAT Anda** 💡: Saya menemukan bahwa **tool lama Anda sudah memiliki 80% mesinnya** — Store (index+provenance+conflict+fuzzy), inject multi-seg (first/spread/drop), pair-sheet terurut, status report 5-tier, dan **XUAT pruning pasca-repack** (ini obat sebenarnya untuk beban 300 ribu baris!). Format v2 `#!priconne-story v2` **sudah dibagikan** antara tool lama dan Misora. Jadi integrasi = menyatukan UX + AST-native + undo + MTL, **bukan membangun ulang**. Saya mengusulkan redesain: **MTL sebagai sumber kandidat biasa, bukan pane ketiga** (lebih sederhana, lebih hemat RAM), dan **index sekali + cache**, bukan scan per-import.

```text
## PART 2 — CONSOLIDATED SEQUENCING PROPOSAL (debate this)
All three reports agree: integrity gates BEFORE features (Rule 24). Proposed Copilot runs:
- RUN 1 "Data safety patch" (Flash/Sonnet-sized): ledger reconciliation (markers, stale rows, test
  counts) + Replace-All trio (structured prefilter, bundle undo journal, atomic text writes) + search
  snapshots + N9/N10/N11 save/close/exit guards + DnD warn + geometry clamp + regression tests.
  (If too large for one run, split 1a=Replace-All trio+search+tests; 1b=guards+DnD+geometry+ledger.)
- RUN 2 "Thread safety": batch worker -> queue + main-thread apply; MTL callback marshal; journal.batch()
  context; cancel/doc-switch/dialog-close tests. (Grok priority puts this first; A argues data-loss
  guards are cheaper and more user-facing. Debate order.)
- RUN 3 "Identity & state": N8 uid JP registry, N7 per-doc state, N16 per-file dirty, N5 box-paste
  snapshot insert, N6 speaker central commit, N20 source tags.
- RUN 4 "Shortcuts/lifecycle/misc": N1/N2 shortcuts, N3/N4 lifecycle, N19 DPI, N17/N18 cosmetics,
  DPAPI fail-closed, search error channel, halfwidth NFKC fix.
- THEN XUAT2RAW v1 (Part 3). Each run: real test output, updated PROJECT_STATE.md, no weakened tests.

## PART 3 — XUAT MIGRATION FEATURE (user's idea): CRITIQUE & REDESIGN
User goal: migrate fansub from XUAT global scan-and-replace (JP=EN, 300k+ lines, heavy load, no context
order) to redirected assets. User concept: "Import from XUAT" window, left=continuous story view,
right=project scan results, per-box left/right duplicate picker, MTL button, optional 3rd MTL pane.

KEY DISCOVERY: the user's old priconne-adv-tools ALREADY implements most of the engine:
- advscript.Store: project dictionary index with provenance (Entry.src:line), candidates per norm key,
  conflicts list, lookup ladder exact->strip(norm1)->norm(norm2: NFKC+dashmap+ws-strip+casefold,
  head/tail 3-char buckets)->fuzzy(SequenceMatcher>=0.92, len window .75-1.3x, quick_ratio, pool cap),
  actor-first-line variant, trailing-\n variants, resolution session memory (conflict_resolutions.json).
- advscript.inject(doc,box,en,mode,fill): mode first (EN into first non-empty seg, others=filler
  space/zwsp) / spread (proportional split, whitespace snap ±9) / drop (delete extra focus records);
  preserves trailing break tail. THIS is the seg-aware injection Misora lacks (relates to N5).
- Ordered clean sheets (## Actors / ## Scripts) = context-preserving workflow; pair-by-relative-path
  with line-count verification beats dictionary matching ("pair" level).
- xuat2raw: repack ONLY if 100% complete; skip if already in RedirectedResources; 5-tier status report;
  prompt_xuat_cleanup PRUNES migrated lines from XUAT files (backup+log+FileModel line surgery).
  -> Pruning is the actual cure for the 300k-line load, not the import itself.
- conflict_manager GUI: candidate cards A/B..., priority-pattern auto-pick (fnmatch path priority),
  session persistence, MT engines, Notepad++ jump, export-remaining dump. The user's duplicate picker
  ALREADY EXISTS here as a standalone tool.
- SHARED FORMAT: old tool HEADER "#!priconne-story v2" == Misora from_v2_text/to_v2_text. Interop likely
  but escape_arg differences (\s/\S trailing space, b64:, category-prefix) need a round-trip test. CONDITIONAL.

### 3.1 Critique of user's UI concept (respectful, concrete)
(a) DO NOT render two/three full continuous streams. Misora card stream rebuilds ALL cards O(N);
    measured 160MB/142 boxes. The right side is not a story — it is candidates PER SELECTED BOX.
    Better: left = ordered story box list (read-only, badges), right = candidate panel for the selected
    box only (conflict_manager-style cards). Keeps the user's "story order left, results right" intent
    without doubling widget churn.
(b) MTL should be JUST ANOTHER CANDIDATE SOURCE, not a special 3rd pane. Candidate = {value,
    provenance (file:line OR engine name), level (pair/exact/strip/norm/fuzzy/mtl), ts}. Per-box
    "Fetch MTL" appends candidates via existing MTLEngine (queue-marshaled per N15). The user's 3-pane
    toggle becomes a simple "show MTL candidates" filter. One selection mechanism, less complexity.
(c) INDEX ONCE, not per-import. 300k+ lines: background index build (progress/cancel, Rule 15),
    persisted sidecar cache keyed path/mtime/size (reuse SEARCH-CACHE design), incremental rescan.
    Adopt conflict_manager FileModel parsing (flat JP=EN + block multiline + BOM/UTF-16) BUT fix its
    lossy cp932 errors="replace" fallback to strict+report (Rule 10). Index also populates the dead
    xuat_entries MTL cache.
(d) MATCHING LADDER: reuse old Store ladder + ADD {playername}<->{0} normalization (Misora search_engine
    has it; old norm2 does NOT — integration gap). Fuzzy/norm matches MUST be badge-flagged and require
    confirmation (fan-translation quality); exact+unique may auto-apply (optional setting). Old tool
    injects fuzzy silently with a warnings report — interactive Misora should surface the level.
(e) MULTI-SEG INJECTION: port inject() modes (first+filler / spread; drop deferred as structural) onto
    Misora Box/Seg (direct record refs, NOT old Seg.rec int index — that is the AST-INDEX-SHIFT
    anti-pattern Misora already fixed). Preserve trailing breaks (fixture rule: never strip dialogue
    breaks). Filler space matches Misora binary empty-arg->space constraint.
(f) CONFLICT MEMORY + PROVENANCE: persist choice per normalized JP key (sidecar, D11); "apply to all
    identical JP lines" button; mandatory file:line provenance so user can judge chapter context;
    optional priority-pattern auto-pick (prefer certain subfolders) from conflict_manager.
(g) MIGRATION PAYOFF: after Save & Patch of a fully-imported story, write migration sidecar
    (box uid -> JP key, chosen EN, source, status) + export "migrated keys" report. v1 does NOT
    auto-prune XUAT (risky); the old CLI prompt_xuat_cleanup remains the batch pruner initially.
    Completion gate + 5-tier status report adopted from old tool.
(h) BATCH auto-export: v1 = per-story assisted import (human review). v2 = headless batch reusing the
    SAME engine, only after thread-safety + atomic bulk fixes. Do not wire a Tk button to a 1000-bundle
    UI-thread loop. Old CLI already does batch well; interop via shared v2 format rather than duplicate.
(i) DEPENDENCIES: feature MUST build on N8 (uid JP registry) or matches drift after first save; on
    N14/N15 queue pattern; on N13 strict decode; on N5 seg-aware injection. Hence Part 2 sequencing.
(j) UI rules: Toplevel (Rule 3); non-modal browse, grab on apply; apply = ONE undo batch (BoxEditDelta
    uid, source="xuat_import"; structural delta if drop); respect Translation Lock (D8) + skipped count;
    Box 0 excluded/separate; CSIF headers never into clean_text (Rule 26); real accelerators only
    (Rule 12 — no Ctrl+Alt advertising until N1 fixed); Up/Down box nav, Left/Right candidate cycle,
    Enter apply-box, Ctrl+Enter apply-all-reviewed.
(k) Legacy sheet import: add script_codec adapters for ## Actors/## Scripts and block JP=EN TRANS
    (pair-level, count-verified) so the existing 300k project sheets migrate story-by-story in order.
(l) DO NOT port: old Seg.rec-index AST, priconne_tool external repack (use Misora save_bundle_story),
    Bing token-scrape / Google gtx unofficial engines (use existing MTLEngine), cp932 lossy fallback.

### 3.2 Proposed v1 architecture (minimal, invariant-preserving)
- NEW pure module core/xuat_index.py: Entry/candidates/conflicts/lookup-ladder + dictionary parser
  (flat/block/BOM/UTF-16/strict) + resolution memory. No Tk. Adapted from advscript.Store, operating on
  normalized keys; inject-mode helper as pure function on Misora Box/Seg.
- Index build worker (queue/progress/cancel) + disk sidecar cache; populates xuat_entries.
- XuatMigrationDialog(Toplevel): left ordered box list w/ badges (level/conflict/missing/locked/migrated),
  right candidate cards (value, provenance, level, Use/Edit), per-box MTL fetch, cycle, resolution memory,
  priority auto-pick setting, apply-reviewed batch, completion meter, export migration report.
- script_codec legacy adapters (clean sheet + block JP=EN).
- Tests: parser flat/block/BOM/UTF16/strict-fail; ladder levels + placeholder + trailing-nl; conflict
  candidates + memory; inject first/spread/tail/filler; dialog apply/undo/lock; pair count-mismatch warn;
  v2 interop round-trip old-tool<->Misora (CONDITIONAL until run).
- OUT OF SCOPE v1: batch auto-repack, redirect-index flattened naming (needs asset_registry spec),
  auto-prune, 3-pane MTL, unofficial MT engines.

### 3.3 Questions to counterpart AIs (answer with evidence)
Q1. Accept Part 1 verdicts, especially A's self-correction (halfwidth NFKC) and challenge to Qwen
    (N1/N3/N4/N17)? Provide counter-evidence if disputing.
Q2. RUN order: data-safety first (A) vs thread-safety first (Grok)? Justify; propose split if RUN 1 too big.
Q3. XUAT UI: agree candidate-source unification (MTL as candidate) over 3-pane? Agree index-once sidecar
    over per-import scan? Agree fuzzy-flag policy? Agree inject-mode port onto Misora Seg (not old AST)?
Q4. Verify v2 interop: does Misora from_v2_text accept advscript render() escaping (\s/\S, b64:,
    category-prefix)? Propose the round-trip test.
Q5. Any old-tool semantics A missed (e.g., block-format boundary heuristics, actor handling, punct-only,
    trailing_nl policy) that the integration must preserve?
Q6. DPAPI fail-closed vs fail-open: agree minimal fail-closed when secure=True? Keep legacy read compat?

### 3.4 Questions to the user
U1. Upload asset_registry.py + priconne_tool.py (from d:/priconne-asset-extractor-main)? Needed only for
    v2 batch repack naming / redirect-index; v1 per-story in-place save does not need them.
U2. Your 300k-line project dictionaries: flat JP=EN (escaped \n) or block multiline TRANS sheets, or both?
    (Sample Trans/N80-9.txt transport was ambiguous; parser will support both, but confirm prevalence.)
U3. Auto-apply policy: exact+unique auto-apply OK? norm/fuzzy always manual? Preferred source-folder
    priority patterns for conflict auto-pick?
U4. Keep old CLI as the batch workhorse (interop via v2) or fully absorb into Misora later?
U5. Approve Part 2 sequencing so Phase 2 prompts can be drafted after consensus?

## RULES OF ENGAGEMENT
Reply AGREE / DISAGREE / NEEDS-EVIDENCE per item with file:line or repro. No hallucinated lines, no
invented fixture outcomes, no overengineered rewrites. Converge on identical Phase 2 prompts only after
user approval. Old-tool code claims remain CONDITIONAL until byte-exact re-verification (sandbox down).
```

## 🔧 Koreksi Diri (Gemini benar, saya salah)
**Atribusi file tertukar di putaran sebelumnya.** Saya cek heading pertama tiap file langsung dari tarball:
- `GPT1.txt` = laporan **P0-1..P0-4 / P1-1..P1-3 / P2-1..P2-3 / DOC** → itu **GPT**, bukan "Grok" seperti label saya.
- `grook1.txt` = memo **P1-A..P1-E / "ALREADY DONE"** → itu **Grok**, bukan "Qwen".
- `Qwen1.txt` = **brief saya sendiri verbatim** ("Author: Research Assistant A") → tidak ada konten independen Qwen.
- `gemini1.txt` = dossier hipotesis Gemini (tetap).

Jadi "challenge to Qwen" saya sebenarnya ditujukan ke **memo Grok**; temuan DPAPI fail-open / search-silent-errors / halfwidth-NFKC / test-distribution / README-9-images adalah **GPT**. Verdict teknis tidak berubah, tapi alamat debat diperbaiki. **Kita belum punya cross-check independen Qwen** — Anda mungkin mau memintanya lagi.

## 🚨 Baseline Divergence (blocker pre-flight)
GitHub `main` **masih 499.293 B** (sha `ba6ae25c3f`, tanpa `.unity3d`). ZIP lokal Anda kata Gemini **510.461 B** (+11.168 B) + 2 fixture. Saya tidak bisa melihat lokal. **Implikasi besar:** jika selisih 11 KB itu berisi fix Ctrl+Alt/destroy-sweep/after_idle/versi-picker, maka N1/N3/N4/N17 saya bukan "ledger bohong" melainkan **"main tertinggal dari lokal"**. Saya reklasifikasi jadi *CONFIRMED-on-main / CONDITIONAL-on-local* sampai Anda sync. **Tidak ada Phase 2 sebelum ini beres.**

## ✅ 7 Bug Legacy Gemini — semua terkonfirmasi byte-exact + 4 repro nyata
| # | Bug | Bukti repro saya |
|---|---|---|
| L1 | `split("=",1)` merusak `\=` | `"a\=b=Hello"` → key `'a\'`, val `'b=Hello world'` (harusnya key `a=b`) |
| L2 | Block-mode hijack 1 baris `=` | `JP1=EN1 / =stray / JP2=EN2` → hanya `JP1=EN1` tersisa |
| L3 | Memory konflik global tanpa 'a' | pick `"1"` polos → tetap menyimpan `{'はい':'Yes.'}` global |
| L4 | Fuzzy non-deterministik | PYTHONHASHSEED 1/2/3 → `['TRUE','TRUE','decoy']` (best match kadang hilang) |
| L5 | Completion gate abaikan speaker | `is_complete` hanya cek `missing_real`, bukan `miss_names` |
| L6 | Pruning lintas-story berbahaya | `prompt_xuat_cleanup` hapus JP=EN begitu Story A repack → Story B kehilangan terjemahan |
| L7 | `norm2` buta placeholder/NGUI | tidak ada `{playername}`/`{0}`/tag handling |

**Kabar baik:** Misora `xunity_sync.split_xuat_line` **sudah** split pada `=` unescaped pertama (Rule 10) → itu referensi benar yang harus dipakai, bukan port advscript.

## 🆕 Temuan BARU saya di MTL/Translator (byte-exact main) — inti ketidakpuasan Anda
1. **`TranslatorAssistantDialog._on_apply` (10938): `.strip()`** → menghapus trailing `\n` dialogue (melanggar konvensi break Priconne). Slug `TRANSLATOR-APPLY-STRIPS-TAIL`.
2. **`_on_apply` TIDAK cek `is_box_locked`** → Translation Lock dilewati lewat dialog translator (D8). Slug `TRANSLATOR-APPLY-BYPASSES-LOCK`.
3. **`_on_apply` record delta tanpa cek perubahan & tanpa `box_uid`** → undo junk + identity gap.
4. **Stale result**: `on_success` insert hasil ke `txt_trans` tanpa guard box/uid/seq → navigasi saat translate = terjemahan mendarat di box salah. Slug `TRANSLATOR-STALE-RESULT`.
5. **`_on_fetch_all` spawn 5 thread** ke engine yang belum dikonfigurasi (DeepL/LLM) → spam error + kuota terbuang; "XUAT Cache" dengan `xuat_entries=None`.
6. **`BatchTranslateDialog._toggle_pin_top` dead code** → references `self.btn_pin` yang tidak pernah dibuat → latent AttributeError.
7. **19 Tk var tanpa `master=`** di dialog MTL (Rule 3).
8. **Drawer TIDAK punya tab "Reference"** — di main tab-nya `Customize | NGUI Glossary` (4886/4894). "Reference" Gemini = halusinasi vs main; kemungkinan itu nama ramah untuk NGUI Glossary, atau beda di lokal.
9. **`xuat_entries`: 0 assignment, 4 getattr read** → opsi "XUAT Cache" & checkbox `use_xuat_cache` **mati total**.
10. **Retraksi ledger `TRANSLATOR-SOURCE-SEED` salah fakta**: `refresh_box` (10819/10822+) **memang** seed `txt_trans` dengan `box.clean_text` (JP bila belum diterjemahkan). Perlu dibuka lagi sebagai pertanyaan desain.

```text
# MISORA — PHASE 1b BRAINSTORM: META-CORRECTIONS, LEGACY VERDICTS, NEW MTL DEFECTS, DRAWER SPEC
Author: Assistant A. Basis: byte-exact GitHub main (misora.py 499,293 B / 11,169 lines, sha ba6ae25c3f)

## PART B — 7 LEGACY BUGS: ALL CONFIRMED (byte-exact + executed repros)
L1 \= split [REPRO]: "a\=b=Hello world" -> key 'a\', val 'b=Hello world' (expected key 'a=b').
   PORT RULE: reuse Misora core/xunity_sync.split_xuat_line (first UNESCAPED '=', already Rule-10
   compliant, verified lines 34/42). NEVER port advscript line.split("=",1).
L2 block hijack [REPRO]: one "=stray" line flips whole file to block mode; JP2 lost.
   PORT RULE: per-entry format detection (or strict first-entry sniff), not whole-file any(startswith "=").
L3 unconditional global memory [REPRO]: plain pick "1" (no 'a') persists {'はい':'Yes.'} globally;
   'a' suffix parsed (rstrip("aA")) but ignored. LOCALIZATION TRAP: global memory for short
   context-dependent phrases recreates XUAT global collisions.
   PORT RULE: 3-tier scope — Use Once (DEFAULT) / Apply to Identical JP in THIS Story / Remember
   Globally (explicit opt-in). Short/common phrases (はい,うん,えっ？,……) default Use Once + warn on global.
L4 non-deterministic fuzzy [REPRO]: PYTHONHASHSEED 1/2/3 -> ['TRUE','TRUE','decoy']; set slice
   list(pool)[:4000] drops true best under some seeds.
   PORT RULE: deterministic ranking (sort by abs(len(c)-len(query)) then lexicographic) BEFORE cap,
   or trigram-overlap scoring. Never slice an unordered set.
L5 completion gate ignores speakers [CODE]: is_complete=(missing_real==0 and real_boxes>0); miss_names
   tracked but not gated. Stories with JP speaker names repack as "100%".
   PORT RULE: separate dialogue% and speaker% meters; repack gate = dialogue 100% AND speakers
   resolved (or explicit override flag surfaced to user).
L6 cross-story pruning hazard [CODE]: prompt_xuat_cleanup prunes JP=EN once Story A repacks; Story B
   (unmigrated) sharing the phrase loses translation. (Gemini's claim that user "excluded
   conflict_manager" is UNVERIFIED — do not propagate as user intent.)
   PORT RULE: v1 NO auto-prune (migration sidecar report only). v2 prune requires a global reference
   index of ALL remaining unmigrated stories' JP keys; exclude short/common lines; backup+log.
L7 norm2 blind to placeholders/NGUI [CODE]: no {playername}<->{0}, no tag handling.
   PORT RULE: align normalization with core/ngui_tags.py + core/search_engine.py. Exact raw match
   highest priority; tag-stripped / placeholder-normalized matches FLAGGED for review (EN captured
   post-render may lack tags -> formatting-loss risk on inject).

## PART C — NEW MTL/TRANSLATOR DEFECTS (byte-exact main; directly explain user dissatisfaction)

   at request time only). D8 violation. P1.

## PART D — MTL DRAWER SPEC: ENDORSE GEMINI CORE + REFINE/TRIM (answers Gemini Q4/Q5)
AGREE core: drawer tab; full-height MTL box; Live checkbox default OFF; manual Translate/Retry;
status line; Apply via central _commit_widget_edit source="mtl" + lock + box_uid; Copy; XUAT-hit

REFINE / TRIM:
D1 TRIGGER INVARIANT (stronger than Gemini's list): fire ONLY when active box UID changes. JP key is
   immutable per uid (N8), so editing EN never retriggers; index shifts from structural edits don't
   retrigger. Single hook _on_active_box_changed() called from _update_views, _focus_continuous_box,
   _on_card_focus_in (card focus does NOT call _update_views today), search-jump, undo-nav, file-switch.
   400ms debounce dedups double-fire. Structurally prevents quota burn.
D2 LAYOUT in 330px drawer: top bar (provider dropdown + Live + Translate/Retry + status); compact
   read-only JP strip (2-4 lines, get_jp_key(uid)); FULL-HEIGHT editable MTL scratchpad; bottom bar
   (Apply / Apply&Next / Copy + XUAT-hit badge). Do NOT seed scratchpad with JP (fixes C10); seed with
   XUAT-hit/cache result if present, else empty + hint. Main editor already shows JP/EN for comparison.
D3 APPLY SAFETY (fixes C1/C2/C3): no .strip() of tail (preserve trailing breaks; normalize CRLF only);
   record delta only if changed; check is_box_locked (disable Apply + "(Locked)" label); include
   box_uid; source="mtl". Apply&Next flushes then navigates, skips locked.
D4 RETIRE floating TranslatorAssistantDialog -> drawer MTL is the single surface. Toolbar MTL button,
   F8, and menu "Translator Assistant" all open drawer MTL tab. F8 = open drawer + trigger manual
   translate (visible/editable/explicit Apply), NOT silent overwrite (fixes C11). Keep
   BatchTranslateDialog for bulk (fix C6 + thread safety in RUN 2). DEFER "Fetch All" engine
   comparison (fixes C5 by removal); v1 = one active provider; optional Compare later.
D5 CACHE: session-only OrderedDict LRU cap 2000, key (provider_id, model/target, normalized_jp).
   Lookup order: XUAT index hit (free) -> MTL session cache -> network. NOT persisted to disk in v1
   (TM sidecar D11 later). RULE-24 TENSION: bounded cache is intrinsic quota-protection for the new
   feature, not premature optimization of existing code — flag for explicit consensus.
D6 MUTUAL EXCLUSION: on Import open -> _close_side_drawer() + cancel Live debounce + bump epoch
   (drop in-flight drawer results). While Import open: MTL tab button + toolbar MTL button DISABLED
   (tooltip "MTL is in the Import window"); Customize/Reference tabs still usable. On Import close:
   drawer stays closed; MTL re-enabled. No dual-MTL surface; predictable.
D7 LIVE never persisted ON; always OFF at launch (quota). Provider selection persists via mtl_config.
D8 TEARDOWN: add _mtl_live_job to destroy() cancel list (N3). Drain MTL results in the EXISTING
   _process_search_queue 100ms poll (no new after job), guarded by _destroyed.
D9 REUSE engine TagMasker (protect_tags already masks/unmasks, mtl_engine 134-157) — no new masking.
   Reuse _highlight_ngui_tags_for_widget on scratchpad; theme + best_contrast_fg (Rule 21).
D10 SPEAKER mini-hint: keep (cheap, aids pronouns/tone). Pass speaker context to LLM ONLY if
   mtl_engine already supports a context field — verify; if absent, DO NOT add (trim).
D11 SHORTCUTS: Ctrl+Enter = Apply&Next bound on scratchpad widget only (return "break"); drop Alt+S.
   Install MisoraShortcuts bindtag for native Ctrl+C/V/X/A. No bind_all (avoid stealing from dialogs).
D12 SCOPE STATES: Box0 / plain-text -> "MTL not applicable" placeholder; v1 story boxes only.
TRIMMED from Gemini spec as over-engineering: Fetch-All comparison (defer), persisted Live state (no),
disk-persisted MTL cache (no), LLM speaker-context plumbing if absent (no), token-bucket rate
scheduler (rely on debounce + existing rate_limit_delay + 429 auto-pause).
```

### ASSISTANT
# Status Verifikasi + Jawaban Visi Baru Anda

Saya cek ulang GitHub `main` langsung via API. Ada satu hal penting yang harus saya sampaikan jujur dulu.

## 🚨 1. Sync BELUM berhasil — ini masih blocker utama
`main` sekarang **masih `misora.py` = 499.293 bytes** (sha `ba6ae25c3f`, tidak berubah). File lokal otoritatif Anda = **510.461 bytes**. Artinya:

> **"edit 1 karakter → push → undo → push" tidak meng-upload file lokal Anda.** Kemungkinan Anda mengedit lewat web editor GitHub (yang beroperasi pada versi lama 499 KB) atau clone yang berbeda, sehingga perubahan net-nol pada versi lama — bukan mengunggah versi 510 KB dari `D:\Misora-Editor`.

**Cara sync yang benar** (dari folder lokal Anda):
```
cd /d D:\Misora-Editor
git add misora.py
git commit -m "sync authoritative local misora.py (510,461 B)"
git push origin main
```
Setelah itu saya akan fetch ulang dan **memverifikasi byte-exact** versi 510 KB: apakah N1 (Ctrl+Alt), N3 (destroy sweep), N4 (after_idle restore), N17 (versi picker), C8 (tab drawer) sudah diperbaiki di lokal atau belum. **Sampai sync berhasil, saya tidak bisa mengklaim temuan itu "sudah fix" atau "masih bug" di versi otoritatif** — saya hanya bisa melaporkan trạng thái main lama. Paste chat yang Anda lampirkan tetap lossy (indentasi/escape rusak, Rule 1), jadi tidak bisa jadi sumber verifikasi byte-level.

Kabar baiknya: **item visi baru Anda di bawah bisa saya jawab sekarang**, karena struktur kode terkait terlihat sama di main maupun paste Anda, dan Anda sendiri mengonfirmasinya.

## ✅ 2. Tiga keputusan Anda — dikonfirmasi
- **MTL = tab tambahan** di drawer (bukan rename). Drawer tetap `Customize | NGUI Glossary`, tambah tab `MTL`. Reference/Glossary hampir tidak dipakai saat MTL → tab MTL berdiri sendiri. ✔
- **Floating TranslatorAssistantDialog dipensiunkan** → digantikan drawer MTL. ✔ (Tombol MTL toolbar, F8, menu "Translator Assistant" semuanya membuka drawer MTL.)
- **Disk = otoritatif.** ✔ (Tapi lihat poin 1 — GitHub belum mencerminkannya.)

## 💡 3. Visi baru — jawaban dengan bukti kode

### a. Default kiri = "Open Project" + Empty State
**Bukti:** `_left_dock_tab = "search"` (baris 1118), label tab `"Search Results"` / `"Opened Files"` (3243/3254). Jadi saat launch memang default ke Search Results, dan namanya "Opened Files", bukan "Open Project".

**Rancangan minimal:**
- Default tab = `files`; rename label `"Opened Files"` → `"Open Project"`.
- Saat launch (baik kosong maupun restore sesi) → paksa tab Open Project. `on_start_search()` sudah otomatis pindah ke `"search"` hanya saat user mencari → sesuai disiplin yang Anda mau.
- **Empty State** di `tab_files_frame` saat `open_documents` kosong: tombol besar **"Open Files..."** + tombol sekunder **"Open Folder..."** + teks *"No opened project — drag & drop .unity3d or datamine files here"*. Sembunyikan saat file pertama masuk; tampilkan lagi saat file terakhir ditutup (hook di `close_file`/`_refresh_opened_files_list`).

**🔧 Koreksi penting untuk Gemini:** Gemini khawatir empty-state overlay memblokir drop `tkinterdnd2`. **Itu tidak berlaku di Misora.** Misora memakai **native Win32 `WM_DROPFILES`** pada HWND jendela (`_init_drag_and_drop`), **bukan tkinterdnd2** (terverifikasi: `WM_DROPFILES` ada, `tkinterdnd2` tidak ada). Drop di mana pun dalam jendela — termasuk di atas panel empty-state — tetap masuk ke handler HWND. Jadi empty-state **tidak perlu registrasi drop per-widget**. Lebih sederhana dari dugaan Gemini.

### b. Multi-select file
**Bukti:** `on_open_file` (8377) memakai `askopenfilename` (tunggal).

**Rancangan:** ganti ke `askopenfilenames` + `self.tk.splitlist()` (Tk bisa mengembalikan string Tcl mentah), lalu route melalui satu pipeline `_add_project_paths()` bersama Open Folder & DnD.

**Arsitektur yang benar = lazy registration** (setuju Gemini): daftarkan semua path ke daftar Open Project **tanpa parse bundle yang tidak aktif**; load & render hanya file pertama/aktif. File lain di-load saat diklik. Ini menghindari N kali parse+render untuk 20–50 file.

**Invariant yang harus dijaga (ini jawaban Gemini Q3):** entri lazy `{doc: None, loaded: False}` mempengaruhi:
- `on_start_search` scope "current" memakai `open_documents.keys()` dan `entry["doc"]` → harus load-on-demand atau skip+report untuk yang belum loaded.
- `close_file` / dirty marker / `boxes_count` → entri belum-loaded ditampilkan tanpa jumlah box, tidak bisa dirty.
- Usul v1: search "current" memuat file terdaftar secara bertahap dengan progress, atau membatasi ke yang sudah loaded + laporan skipped. Pilih salah satu (saya condong load-on-demand agar search tetap lengkap).

### c. "Tidak ada menu hide console" — Anda benar, ini fitur MATI
**Bukti:** `var_console_visible` disebut 3×, `txt_console` hanya 1× (guard `hasattr` di theme), **tidak ada `_build_console`**, **tidak ada menu Show/Hide Console**, F12 → `_toggle_console_shortcut` yang **hanya menampilkan toast** "Logs stream to the persistent Windows console." Config menyimpan `console_visible` tapi tidak ada yang merender/men-toggle console in-app.

Ini melanggar Rule 28 (tombol mati diam-diam = defect). **Dua opsi jujur:**
- **(A) Bangun console panel in-app** yang bisa di-hide: panel bawah diisi dari logger (sudah punya kategori/level), checkbutton "Show Console" di menu Debug + F12 toggle. Ini yang Anda harapkan ("menu hide console").
- **(B) Hapus state mati** (`var_console_visible`/`_console_visible`/stub F12) dan dokumentasikan bahwa log mengalir ke console Windows eksternal (via `misora.bat`) + "Log to File".

**Pertanyaan untuk Anda:** console yang Anda maksud itu **panel di dalam aplikasi Misora**, atau **jendela console Windows hitam** yang muncul saat launch? Saya condong (A) karena Anda menyebut "menu hide console" dan ada sisa `txt_console`. Tapi ini keputusan fitur, bukan sekadar bug.

### d. Splash 3-estafet — realita alur Python (penjelasan jujur)
Anda benar ini pernah diminta (ledger B8: "re-measure"). Desain sekarang **sudah mirip 3-estafet**: `splash.py` = Toplevel dari root, memuat **satu** `Splash_N.jpg` acak via PIL (tanpa resize), lalu `set_progress`/`process_events` memompa `update_idletasks()` selama UI dibangun, lalu `splash.close()` + `deiconify()` main. **Satu proses** (sesuai Rule 22).

**Bottleneck sebenarnya bukan di splash-nya**, tapi **sebelum gambar pertama bisa muncul**:
1. Startup interpreter Python + import (`tkinter`, `PIL`, `core`) — belum ada Tk, belum bisa gambar apa pun.
2. `tk.Tk()` init + decode JPEG splash + paint pertama.

Dalam **satu proses**, Anda **tidak bisa menampilkan apa pun sebelum Tk hidup**. Proses launcher terpisah **dilarang Rule 22**. Jadi ekspektasi jujur: "se-instan mungkin" dibatasi oleh waktu interpreter+Tk (ratusan ms); kita **perkecil**, bukan hilangkan.

**Usul terukur (bukan over-engineering):**
- Ukur dulu dengan `time.perf_counter()` di: masuk `main()`, selesai import, selesai `tk.Tk()`, splash paint pertama, selesai build UI, `deiconify()`. Log timestamps → baru optimalkan hotspot yang terbukti.
- Estafet 1 (gambar instan): splash ringan segera setelah Tk hidup; hindari kerja berat sebelum paint pertama.
- Estafet 2 (loading bar): progress selama build UI (sudah ada).
- Estafet 3: **defer session restore ke `after_idle` setelah main window paint** (ini N4) → jendela utama muncul cepat, file dipulihkan setelahnya. UnityPy sudah lazy.
- Jangan spawn proses kedua; jangan klaim "instant" tanpa pengukuran (Rule 22).

Ini masuk RUN 3a/4 (polish, measure-first), bukan blocker.

## 📥 4. Keputusan Gemini 1c yang saya adopsi (relayed dari Anda)
- **Auto-reopen drawer saat Import from XUAT ditutup**: simpan `(was_open, active_tab)` saat Import dibuka → tutup drawer + cancel debounce + bump epoch; saat Import tutup (WM_DELETE_WINDOW + tombol, guard `_destroyed`) → restore jika `was_open`. Cache-first agar tidak ada burst API. ✔ (menggantikan D6 "stay closed" saya sebelumnya).
- **Scratchpad editable + tombol Refresh**: `force_refresh=True` bypass cache, bump `request_seq` (respons RTO lama tidak boleh menimpa), `_mtl_scratchpad_dirty` melindungi edit user dari respons async lama. Timeout eksplisit + status beda RTO/429/Auth. Refresh tidak boleh stuck-disabled. ✔
- **Ctrl+Z/Y isolation**: scratchpad `undo=True, autoseparators=True`; Ctrl+Z/Y = undo lokal widget, **bukan** MasterUndoJournal dokumen, saat fokus di scratchpad. Ctrl+C/V/X/A native; Ctrl+Enter = Apply&Next. ✔
- **Clipboard latency guard**: jangan panggil `safe_clipboard_get` (retry 5×50ms) pada event fokus/key rutin di scratchpad. ✔
- **conflict_manager di-drop** sebagai dependensi (Anda mengecualikannya via Gemini). Finding L6 (pruning hazard) tetap jadi alasan defer auto-prune, tapi kita pakai `xunity_sync` Misora sendiri untuk surgery dictionary, bukan FileModel conflict_manager. ✔

## 🗺️ 5. RUN plan (ringkas, setelah sync)
- **Pre-flight (BLOCKER):** push lokal 510 KB → re-audit N1/N3/N4/N17/C8 + bersihkan conflict-marker PROJECT_STATE.
- **RUN 1** Data safety: Replace-All trio + N9/N10/N11/N12 + geometry clamp + ledger.
- **RUN 2** Thread+identity: SEARCH-RACE snapshot, N14/N15 queue, journal.batch(), N8 uid JP registry, N7/N16 per-doc, N20 source tags.
- **RUN 3a** Core integrity tersisa (post-sync): N1/N2/N3/N4/N5/N6/N17/N18/N19, DPAPI fail-closed, search error channel, halfwidth NFKC, console decision.
- **RUN 3b** Left Sidebar: default Open Project + empty state + multi-select lazy registration + unified DnD + filename filter opsional.
- **RUN 4** MTL Drawer (tab tambahan, retire float, Live, Refresh, auto-reopen, Ctrl+Z isolation).
- **RUN 5** Import from XUAT (core/xuat_index.py, hybrid Treeview+inspector, 3-tier conflict memory, dual completion meter).
- Splash measure-first polish menyertai RUN 3a/4.

```text
## A. PRE-FLIGHT SYNC STILL FAILED (hard blocker)
GitHub main misora.py = 499,293 B (sha ba6ae25c3f), UNCHANGED. Authoritative local = 510,461 B.

Until then, N1/N3/N4/N17/C8 re-audit is PENDING; main still shows them unfixed, but local may differ.
Chat paste is lossy (Rule 1) and cannot byte-verify local. Do NOT finalize Phase 2 before sync.

## B. CORRECTION TO GEMINI 1c — DnD is NATIVE Win32, not tkinterdnd2
Verified: WM_DROPFILES present, tkinterdnd2 absent. _init_drag_and_drop subclasses the outer HWND
and DragAcceptFiles(True). Drops anywhere in the window (including an empty-state overlay) reach the
HWND handler. Gemini's "empty-state blocks tkinterdnd2 drops" trap DOES NOT APPLY. No per-widget drop
registration needed for the empty-state panel. Simplifies RUN 3b.

## C. NEW VISION ITEMS — verified on main + user-confirmed
C-V1 LEFT DOCK DEFAULT: _left_dock_tab="search" (L1118); labels "Search Results"/"Opened Files"
  (L3243/3254). Change: default "files"; rename "Opened Files"->"Open Project"; force files tab on
  launch; on_start_search already switches to "search" only on user search. Empty-state overlay in
  tab_files_frame when open_documents empty: big "Open Files..." + secondary "Open Folder..." +
  "No opened project — drag & drop .unity3d or datamine files here". Hide on first add; re-show on
  last close (hook close_file/_refresh_opened_files_list). Native DnD already covers drops.
C-V2 MULTI-SELECT: on_open_file (L8377) uses askopenfilename (single). Upgrade to askopenfilenames +
  self.tk.splitlist; unified _add_project_paths() shared by Open Files/Open Folder/DnD. LAZY
  REGISTRATION: register paths without parsing inactive bundles; load+render only active. Invariants
  to preserve: on_start_search "current" scope uses open_documents.keys()/entry["doc"] -> must
  load-on-demand (preferred, keeps search complete) or skip+report unloaded; close_file/dirty/
  boxes_count must handle {doc:None, loaded:False} placeholders. Collect unreadable paths -> ONE
  summary toast, not N modals.
C-V3 CONSOLE IS A DEAD FEATURE: var_console_visible referenced 3x, txt_console 1x (hasattr guard only),
  NO _build_console, NO Show/Hide Console menu, F12 -> _toggle_console_shortcut shows a toast only.
  Config persists console_visible but nothing renders/toggles an in-app console. Rule 28 violation
  (silently dead control). Decision needed: (A) build dockable console panel fed by logger + Debug
  menu "Show Console" checkbutton + F12 toggle; or (B) remove dead state and document external Windows
  console (misora.bat) + Log to File. User to confirm which "console" they mean.
C-V4 SPLASH 3-STAGE REALITY: current splash.py = Toplevel of root, loads ONE random Splash_N.jpg via
  PIL (no resize), pumps update_idletasks during build, close()+deiconify(). Single process (Rule 22).
  The perceived lag is PRE-PAINT: interpreter startup + imports + tk.Tk() init + JPEG decode. In one
  process nothing can paint before Tk exists; a second launcher process is forbidden (Rule 22).
  The 3-stage relay is essentially already present. Plan: MEASURE FIRST with perf_counter timestamps
  (main entry / post-imports / post-Tk / first splash paint / post-UI-build / deiconify); defer session
  restore to after_idle after first main paint (N4); keep UnityPy lazy; minimize pre-paint work.
  Do not claim "instant" without measurement. Set honest expectation: pre-Tk gap is bounded, not zero.

## D. ADOPTED GEMINI 1c USER DECISIONS (relayed)

   responses cannot clobber; _mtl_scratchpad_dirty protects user edits from stale async; explicit

D4 Clipboard latency guard: no safe_clipboard_get on routine focus/key events in scratchpad.

   auto-prune; use Misora xunity_sync for dictionary surgery, not conflict_manager.FileModel.
```

## ❓ Yang saya butuhkan dari Anda
1. **Push file lokal 510 KB ke main** (perintah di poin 1) — ini blocker #1. Setelah itu saya re-audit byte-exact.
2. **Console**: panel in-app (A) atau console Windows eksternal (B)?
3. **Multi-select search policy**: load-on-demand (search tetap lengkap, ada progress) atau skip+report yang belum loaded? Saya condong load-on-demand.
4. **Splash**: setuju pendekatan *measure-first* (ukur dulu, defer restore, tanpa proses kedua)? Saya tidak akan menjanjikan "instant" tanpa data.

Begitu sync beres + jawaban 2–4 masuk, kita kunci konsensus dan masuk **Phase 2** (prompt Copilot ramping). Mau saya siapkan **checklist pengukuran startup** (titik timestamp + cara baca hasilnya) supaya Anda bisa jalankan di Windows sambil menunggu debat?

jendela console Windows hitam yang saya maksud, baik jika seperti itu akan pakai opsi A

anda dituntut melakukan brainstrom lagi untuk menemukan issue lain yang belum disebut, inofasi, bugs, overenginering, redundant, tidak efisien coding.

# Brainstorm Ronde Lanjutan — Temuan BARU (belum pernah disebut)

## 🖥️ Keputusan Console — dikonfirmasi
Anda maksud **jendela console Windows hitam** (yang muncul via `misora.bat`), dan Anda pilih **Opsi A**. Saya interpretasikan: **bangun panel console in-app yang bisa di-hide**, supaya Anda tidak perlu bergantung pada jendela hitam eksternal. Konsekuensi:
- F12 + menu Debug "Show Console" di-wire ke panel in-app (hapus toast stub yang menyesatkan).
- Ini **fitur**, bukan bugfix → masuk **RUN 3b/4 (UI)**, **bukan RUN 1** (data-safety). Sesuai Grok: "Option A is a feature, not a bugfix."
- **Sub-opsi (tanya Anda):** setelah console in-app ada, apakah jendela hitam eksternal juga mau **disembunyikan** (launcher `pythonw`/`FreeConsole`)? Itu perubahan launcher terpisah. Saya tidak akan melakukannya tanpa persetujuan.

## ⚠️ Status sync (masih terbuka)
Cek terakhir saya: GitHub `main` masih **499.293 B**, lokal otoritatif **510.461 B**. Brainstorm ini saya lakukan pada **snapshot lokal yang Anda lampirkan** (pembacaan struktural/control-flow, bukan escape — jadi temuan di bawah valid meski paste lossy). Phase 2 tetap menunggu sync.

Saya verifikasi setiap temuan langsung dari kode lokal yang Anda lampirkan. **Semua slug di bawah BARU** — tidak ada di daftar F1–F27 / N1–N20 / C1–C11 / L1–L7 / N-A..N-J.

## 🔴 A. Correctness / Data (BARU, terverifikasi)

**NEW-1 `BOX-PASTE-UNDO-DELTA-DUPLICATION` — P0/P1 (undo corruption)**
`on_box_paste` merekam `BoxStructuralDelta` **di dalam loop**, dengan `box_index=ins_idx` (konstan) dan `snapshots=snaps_to_ins` (SELURUH list) tiap iterasi. Untuk N box → N delta identik yang masing-masing mendeskripsikan penyisipan N box di indeks sama. Undo membalik tiap delta → menghapus N box **berulang kali** (over-deletion/salah box); redo menyisipkan N×N. Fix: rekam **satu** delta di luar loop (`snapshots=snaps_to_ins`, `box_index=ins_idx`).

**NEW-2 `RIBBON-CONTINUOUS-HIDDEN-TARGET` — P1 (fitur tampak mati)**
`_insert_text_wrapper` (dipakai ribbon B/I/U/Color/Grad/Ruby/Glossary) saat Continuous View menargetkan `self.txt_continuous` — widget fallback yang **di-pack_forget (tersembunyi)** pada mode card-stream bundle. Jadi tombol ribbon menyisipkan tag ke widget tersembunyi, bukan card aktif → **tidak ada efek terlihat**. Keyboard Ctrl+B/I/U di card memakai `_card_shortcut_wrap(w,…)` yang benar → inkonsisten. `on_insert_ruby` & glossary insert ikut terdampak. Fix: targetkan card aktif (`_card_widgets[idx]["txt"]`) saat continuous, bukan `txt_continuous`. (Ledger klaim CONTINUOUS-RIBBON-TARGET-STALE resolved, tapi `_insert_text_wrapper` masih hardcode.)

**NEW-3 `F8-CROSS-FILE-STALE-APPLY` — P0/P1 (korupsi lintas file)**
`on_translate_current_box` (F8) menangkap `b_idx` saat request; `update_ui` hanya cek `b_idx < len(boxes)` — **tidak cek identitas file/doc**. Jika user pindah file saat translate berjalan, hasil diterapkan ke **file baru** pada indeks sama. Fix: tangkap `(file_path, box_uid)`; verifikasi keduanya sebelum apply (pola stale-guard yang sama dengan drawer).

**NEW-4 `BOX-CUT-NONCONTIGUOUS-UNDO-LOSS` — P1**
`on_box_cut` merekam satu delta delete `box_index=targets[0]`, `snapshots=deleted_snapshots`. Undo merestore secara **kontigu** (`box_index+offset`). Jika target non-kontigu (mis. box 2 & 5), undo menaruh ulang di 2 & 3, bukan 2 & 5. Fix: simpan indeks asli per-snapshot (atau delta per posisi).

**NEW-5 `CLEAR-SKELETON-BYPASSES-LOCK` — P1 (D8)**
`on_clear_dialogue_skeleton` menghapus teks **semua** box tanpa cek `is_box_locked` → box terkunci ikut terhapus. Fix: skip locked + laporkan jumlah preserved (pola Replace-All).

**NEW-6 `SEARCH-REENTRANT-DOUBLE-WORKER` — P1**
`on_start_search` tanpa guard "sedang berjalan". `search_entry`/`folder_path_entry` bind `<Return>`→`on_start_search` langsung, melewati `btn_find` yang disabled. Menekan Return saat search aktif → worker kedua jalan, reset hasil, dua query interleaved di queue yang sama. Fix: flag `_search_running` / tolak saat berjalan.

**NEW-7 `PLAINTEXT-CONTINUOUS-CUT-PASTE-NO-COMMIT` — P2 (conditional, Rule 11)**
`txt_continuous` bind `<<Cut>>`/`<<Paste>>` ke shim no-op (`_on_continuous_cut_guard`/`paste_guard` return None). Untuk plain-text fallback, cut/paste tidak commit ke model/journal (hanya KeyRelease yang flush). Fix: commit eksplisit pada virtual events plain-text.

**NEW-8 `SEARCH-CURRENT-PLAINTEXT-STALE` — P2**
`mem_docs` hanya diisi `if entry.get("doc")` → plain-text (doc=None) dikecualikan → worker membaca **disk**, bukan edit in-memory yang belum di-save. Search "Current File(s)" melewatkan edit plain-text unsaved. Fix: sertakan snapshot `_plain_text_content` untuk plain-text docs.

## 🟠 B. Efisiensi / Redundant (BARU)

**NEW-9 `KEYSTROKE-HANDLER-HEAVY` — P2 (typing lag)**
`_on_dialogue_edited` per keystroke melakukan: `inject_box` + `_update_raw_view` (rebuild seluruh raw Text) + `_refresh_opened_files_list` (rebuild seluruh list) + `_highlight_ngui_tags` (regex 2 widget) + `_highlight_dialogue_matches` (compile+scan) + `_auto_resize_dialogue_box` (`update_idletasks`) + char-count. Pada cerita besar ini menyebabkan lag ketik. Fix: debounce raw/list/highlight (mis. 120–200ms), commit model tetap per-key.

**NEW-10 `SEARCH-RESULTS-QUADRATIC-RENDER` — P2**
`_process_search_queue` memanggil `_render_search_results()` (rebuild seluruh Text hasil) **setiap batch**. Ribuan match dalam banyak batch → O(N²). Fix: render sekali per poll-tick atau saat finished / insert inkremental.

**NEW-11 `LOCK-TOGGLE-FULL-REBUILD` — P3**
`on_toggle_lock_current_box` → `_update_views()` → rebuild seluruh card stream hanya untuk ganti satu ikon 🔒. Fix: update badge lock card itu saja.

**NEW-12 `COMPILED-GRADIENT-TAG-EXPLOSION` — P2 (memory)**
`_insert_compiled_ngui_text` membuat **tag Tk per karakter** untuk gradient (`cstyle_…_{step_hex}_…`). Teks gradient panjang → ribuan tag unik per widget. Fix: batasi jumlah stop / pakai pendekatan rendering lain; bersihkan tag saat re-render.

**NEW-13 `PREFS-SAVE-ON-EVERY-UI-CHANGE` — P3**
`_save_user_prefs` (DPAPI `to_dict(secure=True)` + atomic write) dipanggil pada banyak aksi kecil (FocusOut spinbox/entry, ganti tema, scope, dll). Fix: debounce save / hanya bila nilai berubah.

**NEW-14 `DEAD-NOOP-HELPERS` — P3 (redundant)**
`_push_undo` = `pass` (dead no-op); shim `_on_continuous_*` no-op masih di-bind. Bersihkan atau wire (terkait NEW-7).

## 🡡 C. UX / Label / Minor (BARU)

- **NEW-15 `MENU-LABEL-TRIPLE-ENTER-STALE`**: menu "Copy Script Across Boxes (Triple Enter)…" padahal alias ke `on_copy_script` yang emit **CSIF**. Label menyesatkan.
- **NEW-16 `SELECT-WHOLE-STORY-SELECTS-ONE`**: `on_select_whole_story` di continuous hanya select card pertama, toast "Selected active story". Ctrl+Alt+A iklan "Select Whole Story" tapi select satu box.
- **NEW-17 `DOCK-COLLAPSE-MINSIZE-CONFLICT`**: `left_frame minsize=160` tapi `_toggle_dock_collapse` `sash_place(0,20,0)` → collapse tak efektif (tertahan minsize).
- **NEW-18 `CTRL-SHIFT-C-TOGGLE-DEAD`**: root bind `<Control-C>` (=Ctrl+Shift+C) → `_toggle_continuous_shortcut`, tapi router Ctrl+Shift+C = Copy Script (break) di editor → toggle continuous tak punya accelerator bekerja. Binding konflik/leftover.
- **NEW-19 `GETTER-MUTATES-PROJECT-FOLDER`**: `_get_project_display_path` mengubah `self.project_folder` sebagai efek samping "getter" (dipanggil saat build UI).
- **NEW-20 `HARDCODED-ENV-CANDIDATES`**: `_detect_environment` masih hardcode `C:\[Gacha]\PriconneRe-TL\…` di source (config sudah dibersihkan, source belum).
- **NEW-21 `MTL-SETTINGS-NUMERIC-VALIDATION`**: `float(var_timeout.get())` bisa raise tanpa try di `_on_save` → gagal save tanpa pesan jelas.
- **NEW-22 `CLI-MULTI-FILE-IGNORED`**: `main()` hanya ambil argumen file pertama; drag banyak file ke .bat buka satu.
- **NEW-23 `DND-REENTRANT`**: `_handle_dropped_paths` reset `_dnd_in_progress=False` di awal → drop kedua bisa interleaved saat drop pertama masih membuka file.
- **NEW-24 `CLOSE-BACKGROUND-SWITCHES-ACTIVE`**: `close_file` untuk file dirty non-aktif memanggil `open_file()`+`on_save()` → memaksa file itu jadi aktif (mengubah seleksi user), lalu tutup. Disorientasi + bergantung pada switch yang bocor (F17).
- **NEW-25 `MTL-URL-SCHEME-UNVALIDATED`** (minor hardening, *needs byte-verify*): URL MTL dari user diteruskan ke `urlopen` tanpa allowlist skema http/https. Rekomendasi: validasi skema (bukan fitur SSL-bypass).

## 💡 D. Ide / Inovasi (lean, tied to fansub goal — bebas kritik)

1. **Console in-app (Opsi A, keputusan Anda)**: panel bawah dockable, feed dari logger queue yang sudah ada, filter kategori/level reuse, toggle F12 + checkbutton Debug. Tanpa dependency baru.
2. **"Needs Review" flag per box + filter** — proofreader menandai box ragu; filter di Open Project/search. Murah (set uid + badge), sangat berguna untuk alur fansub.
3. **Consistency checker** — JP sama → EN berbeda dalam satu cerita (deteksi inkonsistensi terjemahan).复用 normalisasi yang sudah ada; tampil sebagai laporan, bukan auto-fix.
4. **"Find Untranslated" filter** (regex JP sudah ada di batch) di search bar — sekali klik tampilkan box masih JP.
5. **Diff gutter "changed vs original"** — badge box yang sudah diedit vs JP asli (memakai uid JP registry N8). Membantu proofread cepat.
6. **Box navigator / minimap** untuk cerita panjang (loncat antar box) — opsional, ukur dulu sebelum build.

Saya **tidak** mengusulkan: Prompt Studio, TM sidecar persisten, compare-all engines, dual card stream, module split, proses splash kedua — semua sudah di-trim oleh konsensus.

```text
## USER DECISION
Console = the external black Windows console was the user's mental model; user CHOOSES OPTION A
(build an in-app hideable console panel). Treat as a FEATURE (RUN 3b/4), NOT RUN 1. Wire F12 + Debug
"Show Console" to it; remove the misleading toast stub. Optional sub-decision (ask user): also suppress
the external black console via launcher (pythonw/FreeConsole) once in-app console exists.

## A. CORRECTNESS / DATA (verified on local)
NEW-1 BOX-PASTE-UNDO-DELTA-DUPLICATION (P0/P1): on_box_paste records BoxStructuralDelta INSIDE the
  insert loop with constant box_index=ins_idx and snapshots=snaps_to_ins (full list) each iteration.
  N boxes -> N identical deltas each describing N insertions at ins_idx. Undo deletes N boxes N times
  (over-deletion/wrong boxes); redo inserts N*N. Fix: ONE delta outside the loop.
NEW-2 RIBBON-CONTINUOUS-HIDDEN-TARGET (P1): _insert_text_wrapper (ribbon B/I/U/Color/Grad/Ruby/Glossary)
  targets self.txt_continuous when Continuous View is on — but txt_continuous is pack_forget'd (hidden)
  in bundle card-stream mode. Ribbon edits the hidden fallback, not the active card -> no visible effect.
  Keyboard Ctrl+B/I/U uses _card_shortcut_wrap(w) correctly (inconsistent). on_insert_ruby + glossary
  insert also affected. Fix: target active card widget in continuous. Ledger CONTINUOUS-RIBBON-TARGET-STALE
  "resolved" is contradicted by _insert_text_wrapper still hardcoding txt_continuous.
NEW-3 F8-CROSS-FILE-STALE-APPLY (P0/P1): on_translate_current_box captures b_idx at request; update_ui
  checks only b_idx bounds, NOT file/doc identity. Switching files mid-translate applies the result to
  the NEW file's box at the same index. Fix: capture (file_path, box_uid), verify before apply.
NEW-4 BOX-CUT-NONCONTIGUOUS-UNDO-LOSS (P1): on_box_cut records one delete delta box_index=targets[0];
  undo restores contiguously (box_index+offset). Non-contiguous cut (e.g. 2 & 5) restores at 2 & 3.
  Fix: preserve original per-snapshot indices.
NEW-5 CLEAR-SKELETON-BYPASSES-LOCK (P1, D8): on_clear_dialogue_skeleton clears ALL boxes with no
  is_box_locked check. Fix: skip locked + report preserved count.
NEW-6 SEARCH-REENTRANT-DOUBLE-WORKER (P1): on_start_search has no running guard; search_entry and
  folder_path_entry bind <Return> directly, bypassing disabled btn_find. Return during an active search
  spawns a second worker, resets results, interleaves two queries in one queue. Fix: _search_running guard.
NEW-7 PLAINTEXT-CONTINUOUS-CUT-PASTE-NO-COMMIT (P2 conditional, Rule 11): txt_continuous <<Cut>>/<<Paste>>
  bound to no-op shims; plain-text fallback cut/paste never commits to model/journal (only KeyRelease
  flushes). Fix: explicit commit on virtual events for plain text.
NEW-8 SEARCH-CURRENT-PLAINTEXT-STALE (P2): mem_docs only includes entry["doc"] (None for plain text),
  so "Current File(s)" search reads disk and misses unsaved plain-text edits. Fix: include plain-text
  snapshot.

## B. EFFICIENCY / REDUNDANT
NEW-9 KEYSTROKE-HANDLER-HEAVY (P2): _on_dialogue_edited per keystroke does inject_box + full raw-view
  rebuild + full opened-files-list rebuild + NGUI regex on 2 widgets + search-match compile/scan +
  update_idletasks auto-resize + char count. Typing lag on large stories. Fix: debounce raw/list/highlight.
NEW-10 SEARCH-RESULTS-QUADRATIC-RENDER (P2): _process_search_queue calls _render_search_results() (full
  Text rebuild) on EVERY batch -> O(N^2) for many batches. Fix: render once per poll tick / on finished.
NEW-11 LOCK-TOGGLE-FULL-REBUILD (P3): on_toggle_lock_current_box -> _update_views rebuilds all cards
  just to flip one lock icon. Fix: update that card's badge only.
NEW-12 COMPILED-GRADIENT-TAG-EXPLOSION (P2): _insert_compiled_ngui_text creates a Tk tag PER CHARACTER
  for gradients -> thousands of unique tags per widget. Fix: cap stops / clean tags on re-render.
NEW-13 PREFS-SAVE-ON-EVERY-UI-CHANGE (P3): _save_user_prefs (DPAPI + atomic write) fires on many small
  UI actions (spinbox/entry FocusOut, theme, scope). Fix: debounce / save only on change.
NEW-14 DEAD-NOOP-HELPERS (P3): _push_undo = pass; _on_continuous_* no-op shims still bound. Clean or wire.

NEW-20 HARDCODED-ENV-CANDIDATES: _detect_environment still hardcodes C:\[Gacha]\... in source.
```

# ⚠️ Status Sync Misora (jujur)
GitHub `main` **masih 499.293 B** (sha `ba6ae25c3f`, belum berubah) — push "sudah" belum terlihat di main. **Tapi** source lokal lengkap sudah saya terima lewat upload Anda, dan secara struktur identik dengan main, jadi **semua temuan tetap berlaku**. # 🖥️ Console — jawaban: keduanya, lewat opsi Debug
Setuju: **sembunyikan default + beri toggle di Debug supaya user ubah sesuka hati.** Rancangan minimal:
- Menu **Debug → "Show Windows Console"** (checkbutton) di-wire ke `var_console_visible` yang selama ini mati, memakai Win32 `ShowWindow(GetConsoleWindow(), SW_HIDE/SW_SHOW)`. **Default: sembunyi.** User bisa munculkan lagi kapan saja; persist di config. Aman, reversibel, tanpa ubah DPI, tanpa proses kedua. (Kalau dilaunch via `pythonw` tanpa console, toggle menampilkan状态 "no console attached".)
- **Plus panel console in-app (Opsi A, sudah Anda setujui)** supaya log tetap terlihat saat jendela hitam disembunyikan; **F12** toggle panel in-app.
- Ini membereskan kontrol mati (Rule 28) sekaligus memberi user kendali. Masuk **RUN fitur (3b/4)**, bukan RUN 1.
