|---|---|---|
| **A1** | Target mock tidak disebut | `load_bundle_story` diimpor ke namespace `misora`. Patch `core.bundle_io.load_bundle_story` **tidak akan** mencegat panggilan di `open_file()` → Test 1 gagal intercept, Copilot bingung |
| **A2** | Flush pakai `focus_get()` implisit | `_get_active_text_widget()` mengembalikan `tk.Text` apa pun yang fokus. Saat `open_file()` dipanggil dari klik dock, fokus bisa di `txt_opened_files` (read-only) → `_commit_widget_edit()` **no-op diam-diam**, Test 5 jadi meaningless |
| **A3** | "Active document open" ambigu | File plain-text punya `current_doc = None` tapi `current_file_path` terisi. Guard harus pakai `current_file_path is not None`, kalau tidak flush plain-text terlewat |
| **A4** | Ekspektasi failing-first per tes | Test 4 (regresi load sukses) **harus LULUS sebelum & sesudah**. Kalau semua 6 dituntut gagal dulu, Copilot bisa "memperbaiki" tes yang sudah benar |
| **A5** | Ekstensi file Test 2 | Harus `.txt` agar masuk cabang text, bukan cabang bundle |
| **A6** | Reload gagal pada path yang sama | Entri `open_documents[path]` yang **sudah ada** tidak boleh terhapus/tertimpa. Draft hanya bilang "jangan buat entri untuk file gagal" |
| **A7** | Determinisme baseline & nama file tes | Beri **dua** ukuran (CRLF 510.461 B / LF 499.293 B — keduanya sudah saya verifikasi) supaya Copilot tidak STOP palsu; hash hanya berlaku **sebelum** edit; nama file tes dibuat eksplit agar perintah verifikasi tidak ambigu; gate Plan-Mode berlaku untuk `misora.py` (S1), bukan untuk edit hygiene S0 |

---

## 📋 FINAL PHASE 2 PROMPT — S0 + S1 (siap salin ke Copilot)

```text
MISORA EDITOR — PHASE 2
ENGINEERING TASK: S0 + S1 (PRE-FLIGHT & STAGED OPEN FILE SAFETY)

ROLE & MODE:
You are a senior Python engineer working on Misora Editor, a Tkinter-based Unity3D story
editing application. Execute this narrowly scoped data-integrity task with minimal, clean
diffs. If operating in Plan Mode: S0 hygiene edits below are pre-authorized; present a brief
plan and wait for approval before modifying misora.py source (S1).

CONTEXT POINTERS (READ BEFORE EDITING):
- AGENTS.md
- PROJECT_STATE.md
- misora.py (specifically MisoraApp.open_file() and its helpers)
- Relevant existing tests under tests/
Do not rely on chat summaries as source code; verify exact lines directly from disk.

==================================================
PART 0 — NON-NEGOTIABLE GUARDRAILS & FILE OWNERSHIP
==================================================
1. Workspace: work strictly in the existing local Windows workspace `D:\Misora-Editor`.
2. Allowed files to create/modify:
   - `.gitattributes`
   - `PROJECT_STATE.md`
   - `misora.py` (scoped to `open_file()` and tightly related helper logic)
   - `tests/test_s1_open_file_safety.py` (new focused regression test file)
3. DO NOT modify:
   - `core/story_ast.py`, `core/bundle_io.py`, `core/undo_journal.py`, or ANY other `core/` module
   - Unrelated UI views, shortcuts, MTL, search, or Replace-All code
   Runtime mocking of core callables inside tests is allowed; editing core source is NOT.
4. Preserve all existing user files and uncommitted work. Never run `git reset`, `git clean`,
   or destructive `git checkout`.
5. Do not create a git commit or push changes.
6. Do not add new dependencies.
7. Do not remove, skip, weaken, or silently mock existing tests to force a pass.
8. Never claim a command or test passed unless you actually executed it and observed the output.
9. Follow all `AGENTS.md` architectural invariants.
10. Stop strictly after S0 and S1. Do not implement S2 or any later slice.

==================================================
S0 — BASELINE VERIFICATION & REPOSITORY HYGIENE
==================================================
Complete and record S0 BEFORE modifying `misora.py`.

A. Verify workspace state and report:
   - Git repository root (expected: `D:\Misora-Editor`)
   - Current branch and HEAD commit hash
   - `git status` (any uncommitted modifications)
   - Exact Python interpreter path and version

B. Verify the `misora.py` PRE-EDIT baseline. Known-good reference values:
   - Windows CRLF form: 510,461 bytes,
     SHA-256 `2188eab968e031d4d5469f117f3cfb8403dc27622b5b6009d38b3880dd97babe`
   - LF-normalized form of the same content: 499,293 bytes
   Compute the raw-byte SHA-256 and the LF-normalized byte count, and confirm the file matches
   this baseline in either line-ending representation. These reference values apply ONLY to
   the pre-edit file; do not re-apply them after S1 changes the file.
   If unexpected SEMANTIC modifications are present, STOP before editing source and report
   them without overwriting anything.

C. Verify runtime config tracking:
   - Check whether `misora_config.json` is tracked by Git (`.gitignore` already excludes it).
   - Never print config contents, API keys, or secrets.
   - If tracked, report it without deleting or automatically untracking it.

D. Add minimal `.gitattributes` with exactly:
```
*.py text
*.bat text eol=crlf
*.unity3d binary
*.asset binary
```
   IMPORTANT:
   - Do NOT force `*.py` to `eol=lf`.
   - Do NOT run `git add --renormalize .` or perform mass line-ending conversions.
   - After writing it, confirm `git status` shows no mass file modification; if it does, STOP
     and report.

E. Resolve `PROJECT_STATE.md` Git conflict markers:
   - Remove unresolved markers (`<<<<<<<`, `=======`, `>>>>>>>`) while preserving valid
     historical information.
   - Do not invent version numbers or unverified test counts.

F. Run baseline checks. Prefer `py -3.12`; if the `py` launcher is unavailable, fall back to the
   active Python 3.12 interpreter. Report the exact command used.
   - `py -3.12 -m compileall -q misora.py core assets tests`
   - `py -3.12 -m unittest discover -s tests -v`
   Record actual total tests, passed, failures, errors, skipped, OS, Python version, and any
   missing dependencies/fixtures. Do not claim the historical 100/100 count unless this run
   confirms it. If the full suite cannot complete for environment reasons (missing UnityPy,
   missing fixture, no display), report that as an environment blocker honestly and STILL run
   the focused S1 tests below.

==================================================
S1 — STAGED OPEN FILE SAFETY (OPEN-FAILURE-STATE-CORRUPTION)
==================================================
TARGET: `misora.py` -> `MisoraApp.open_file()`

ROOT PROBLEM TO FIX:
1. `open_file()` mutates active application state (`current_file_path`, `is_dirty`,
   `_modified_boxes`, `_original_box_texts`, `_locked_boxes`, `_plain_text_content`, window
   title, `recent_files`, `last_opened_dir`, etc.) BEFORE the target file finishes loading and
   parsing. If loading fails, old document/Unity objects remain paired with the failed file's
   path and wiped metadata/locks.
2. Non-bundle files are read with `encoding="utf-8", errors="replace"`, silently corrupting
   invalid UTF-8 bytes into `U+FFFD` as a successful load.

REQUIRED IMPLEMENTATION:

A. Stage Loading & Strict Decoding in Local Variables
   - Load, decode, and parse the target file into local temporary variables without mutating
     any `self.*` document/UI state beforehand.
   - Reuse existing loaders strictly:
     1. Unity bundles (`.unity3d`, `.asset`): reuse `load_bundle_story()`.
     2. Misora v2 scripts: reuse `StoryDocument.from_v2_text()`.
     3. Ordinary plain-text files: strict UTF-8 decoding (`encoding="utf-8"`, NO
        `errors="replace"`, NO `errors="ignore"`).
   - Invalid UTF-8 (`UnicodeDecodeError`), malformed v2 parse errors, and corrupt bundle errors
     MUST all fail cleanly.

B. Pending Edit Flush on Valid Switch (`AGENTS.md` Rule 13)
   - A previous context exists when `self.current_file_path is not None` (this covers story
     documents AND plain-text files where `current_doc is None`). On the very first open, or
     when no previous context exists, skip the flush entirely — do not crash.
   - Perform the flush ONLY after the target is ready to commit: fresh disk loads after staging
     succeeds, AND cache-hit switches when `reload_from_disk=False`. Do NOT flush before a disk
     load succeeds.
   - Resolve the previous document's story editor widget EXPLICITLY before committing new
     state. Do NOT rely on `focus_get()`: when `open_file()` is invoked from a dock/list click,
     focus can be on a read-only widget such as `txt_opened_files` or `txt_results`, which makes
     `_commit_widget_edit()` silently no-op. Resolution order:
       * if `is_box0_active` -> `txt_box0_summary`
       * elif continuous view and `current_box_idx` in `_card_widgets` -> that card's `txt`
       * elif plain-text fallback in continuous view -> `txt_continuous`
       * else -> `txt_dialogue`
     Guard each with `winfo_exists()`, then call `_commit_widget_edit(that_widget)`.
   - If the flush raises, report through the existing logger/error dialog and return WITHOUT
     committing any new state.
   - Preserve existing explicit `reload_from_disk=True` semantics. If a reload from disk fails,
     keep the previous in-memory document and editor buffer intact.
   - Do not invent a new commit architecture.

C. Commit State Together Only After Success
   - Only after staging succeeds, commit all active document fields together:
     `current_file_path`, `current_doc`, `current_unity_env`, `current_text_obj`,
     `is_bundle_file`, `current_box_idx`, `is_box0_active`, `is_dirty`, `_modified_boxes`,
     `_original_box_texts`, `_locked_boxes`, `_plain_text_content`, plus `last_opened_dir`,
     window title, `recent_files`, `open_documents`, and UI views/status.
   - Preserve the existing `open_documents` cache architecture and cache-hit behavior.
   - Do NOT change how `_original_box_texts` is populated on cache-hit in this slice; the
     UID-keyed Japanese source registry is owned by a later slice.

D. Complete Preservation on Any Failure
   - If loading or parsing fails for any reason, leave previous `current_file_path`,
     `current_doc`, `current_unity_env`, `current_text_obj`, `is_bundle_file`, `is_dirty`,
     `_locked_boxes`, `_modified_boxes`, `_original_box_texts`, `_plain_text_content`,
     `current_box_idx`, `is_box0_active`, visible editor buffer, window title, `recent_files`,
     `last_opened_dir`, and `open_documents` completely unchanged.
   - Do not create or overwrite any `open_documents` entry for the failed file.
   - When `reload_from_disk=True` fails for the CURRENT path, the already-existing
     `open_documents[path]` entry must remain intact (neither deleted nor overwritten).
   - Report the error through existing logger and error dialog mechanisms; keep the app usable.

==================================================
REQUIRED REGRESSION TESTS (FAILING-FIRST)
==================================================
Create `tests/test_s1_open_file_safety.py` using the existing `unittest` architecture. Do not
introduce a new testing framework.

Clean TDD order (no git commands needed):
  1. Write the tests below.
  2. Run them against the UNPATCHED code and record the actual output.
  3. Implement S1.
  4. Re-run and record the actual output.

Per-test expectation (do not "fix" a test that is already correct):
  - Tests 1, 2, 3, 5, 6 MUST FAIL before S1 and PASS after S1.
  - Test 4 MUST PASS both before and after S1 (it is a regression guard, not a defect probe).

Mock targets (critical — wrong target will not intercept the call):
  - `load_bundle_story` is imported into the `misora` module namespace, so patch
    `misora.load_bundle_story`. Patching `core.bundle_io.load_bundle_story` will NOT intercept
    the call made inside `open_file()`.
  - For the v2 failure path use
    `unittest.mock.patch.object(misora.StoryDocument, "from_v2_text", side_effect=...)`.
    This is a runtime mock only; never edit `core/story_ast.py`.

TEST 1 — Failed Bundle Load
  - Open valid document A with non-default dirty/lock/modified/source-cache/title/recent state.
  - Attempt to open an existing `.unity3d` path B with `misora.load_bundle_story` mocked to raise.
  - Assert A remains intact across path, doc identity, Unity env/obj, dirty state, locks,
    modified boxes, source cache, title, `recent_files`, and `open_documents` (no entry for B).

TEST 2 — Invalid UTF-8 Real Bytes
  - Open valid document A.
  - Write real invalid UTF-8 bytes to a disk file B with a `.txt` extension, e.g.
    `b"hello\x80\xffworld"` (no BOM; does not start with `#!priconne-story`).
  - Attempt to open B; assert loading fails and A remains active and completely unchanged.

TEST 3 — Malformed / Failing v2 Script
  - Open valid document A.
  - Create a v2 file B and trigger a `StoryDocument.from_v2_text` parse failure. If the parser
    is permissive for crafted text, mock `from_v2_text` to raise instead of modifying core.
  - Assert loading fails and A remains active and completely unchanged.

TEST 4 — Successful Load Regression (must pass before AND after)
  - Verify normal loading still works for ordinary UTF-8 text, a valid Misora v2 script, and a
    valid Unity bundle when its fixture and `UnityPy` are available.

TEST 5 — Pending Edit Preservation
  - Open document A, modify the editor widget WITHOUT triggering `KeyRelease`, then successfully
    switch to document B. Verify BOTH a fresh-load switch and a cache-hit switch.
  - Assert A's edit was committed into A's in-memory document before switching.
  - Repeat with a failing open of invalid B; assert A's uncommitted editor buffer and state are
    NOT replaced or discarded.

TEST 6 — Save Target After Failed Open
  - While A is active, attempt to open invalid B (fails).
  - Verify the active save target remains A and B is never targeted or overwritten.

==================================================
OUT OF SCOPE (DO NOT IMPLEMENT)
==================================================
- Save All, `on_save()` success boolean, Close/Exit lifecycle changes (S2/S3)
- Per-file `UndoJournal` revision redesign or `_dirty_state_changed` refactor (S2/S7)
- Multi-file Replace All (S5)
- UID-based locks or UID-based Japanese source registry (S7)
- Search/MTL/Batch threading changes (S6)
- `destroy()` after-job sweep, shortcuts, ribbon, Player Name button, Watermark (S8)
- Left Sidebar Open Project, In-App Console, MTL Drawer, Import from XUAT (S9–S11)
- Module splitting or unrelated performance/UI rewrites

==================================================
FINAL VERIFICATION & DELIVERABLE
==================================================
After implementing S1, run and report actual outputs:
1. `py -3.12 -m compileall -q misora.py core assets tests`
2. `py -3.12 -m unittest tests.test_s1_open_file_safety -v`
   (show the BEFORE-fix failure output and the AFTER-fix pass output)
3. `py -3.12 -m unittest discover -s tests -v`
4. Inspect `git status` and `git diff` to verify: no mass EOL churn, no unrelated edits, no
   weakened tests, no new dependencies, no conflict markers, and no exposed config/secrets.

Deliver a concise report containing:
1. Root-cause summary
2. S0 baseline verification results
3. Files modified
4. Summary of the staged-loading implementation
5. Regression tests added (with failing-first -> passing proof, and Test 4 pass/pass)
6. Exact commands run and actual outputs
7. Known limitations or skipped tests
8. Items explicitly deferred to S2+
9. `PROJECT_STATE.md` update summary

STOP after S0 + S1 verification and wait for user review.
```

## Setup Eksekusi Copilot (rekomendasi)

- **Surface & Mode:** VS Code Copilot Chat — **Plan Mode → Agent Mode**. Mulai chat baru agar konteks bersih. Biarkan Copilot menyelesaikan **S0** dulu, lihat laporannya, baru approve edit `misora.py`.
- **Model:** bebas sesuai kuota Anda (tidak dikunci). Kalau ada pemilih Fusion, Standard/High cukup untuk slice ini.
- **Steering:** setelah selesai, **review `git diff`** sebelum lanjut ke S2. Jangan biarkan Copilot auto-lanjut.

## Langkah berikutnya

Blok di atas = **draft Gemini + 5 tightening-nya + 7 presisi saya**. Karena aturan Anda menuntut prompt **identik antar-AI**:

1. Kirim **tabel A1–A7** ke GPT & Gemini untuk konfirmasi cepat (semuanya aditif, tidak mengubah scope S0/S1 — seharusnya tidak perlu ronde debat baru).
2. Jika keduanya setuju → blok ini **dikunci sebagai prompt final yang sama persis** → masuk **Phase 3 (Run Copilot)**.
3. Jika ada yang menolak salah satu delta, kirim balik ke saya; saya akan sesuaikan.

Saya **tidak** mengeksekusi apa pun. Phase 3 tetap milik Anda dan Copilot.
