```html
/* ---------- state ---------- */
const S={
  files:[], activeId:null,
  ui:{theme:"violet",font:15,gutter:true,ruler:false,tip:"normal",density:"comfortable",sound:true,
      dockTab:"project",drawerTab:"inspector",dockOpen:true,drawerOpen:false,
      scope:"file",filter:"all",sort:"index",query:"",rawOpen:false},
  sel:new Set(), pinned:null, undo:[], redo:[], views:[], lastEdit:0, dragUid:null
};
const UIKEY="misora-ui-spec-v1";
function persist(){ try{localStorage.setItem(UIKEY,JSON.stringify({ui:S.ui,views:S.views}));}catch(e){} }
function restore(){ try{const r=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(UIKEY)||"{}"); if(r.ui)Object.assign(S.ui,r.ui); if(Array.isArray(r.views))S.views=r.views;}catch(e){} }
const file=()=>S.files.find(f=>f.id===S.activeId)||null;
const box=(f,id)=>f?f.boxes.find(b=>b.uid===id):null;
function curBox(){ const f=file(); if(!f)return null; return box(f,f.view.cur)||f.boxes[0]||null; }
function curIdx(){ const f=file(); const b=curBox(); return f&&b?f.boxes.indexOf(b):-1; }

/* ---------- sound (tied to visible actions, one master mute) ---------- */
let AC=null;
function sfx(kind){
  if(!S.ui.sound)return;
  try{
    AC=AC||new (window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();
    if(AC.state==="suspended")AC.resume();
    const t=AC.currentTime, mk=(f,d,type,g)=>{
      const o=AC.createOscillator(),v=AC.createGain();
      o.type=type||"sine";o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f,t);
      v.gain.setValueAtTime(0,t);v.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(g||.05,t+.008);
      v.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t+d);
      o.connect(v);v.connect(AC.destination);o.start(t);o.stop(t+d+.02);return o;
    };
    if(kind==="click")mk(1180,.05,"sine",.035);
    else if(kind==="toggle"){mk(760,.06,"triangle",.04);setTimeout(()=>sfxRaw(1040,.05),55);}
    else if(kind==="lock")mk(300,.1,"square",.035);
    else if(kind==="unlock")mk(560,.09,"triangle",.04);
    else if(kind==="apply"){[620,830,1050].forEach((f,i)=>setTimeout(()=>sfxRaw(f,.09),i*60));}
    else if(kind==="err"){mk(170,.13,"sawtooth",.03);}
    else if(kind==="jump")mk(880,.06,"sine",.03);
  }catch(e){}
}
function sfxRaw(f,d){ try{ if(!AC||!S.ui.sound)return; const t=AC.currentTime,o=AC.createOscillator(),v=AC.createGain();
  o.type="sine";o.frequency.setValueAtTime(f,t);v.gain.setValueAtTime(.04,t);v.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(.0001,t+d);
  o.connect(v);v.connect(AC.destination);o.start(t);o.stop(t+d+.02);}catch(e){} }

/* ---------- toast ---------- */
function toast(msg,kind="info",action){
  const wrap=$("#toasts"); const el=document.createElement("div");
  el.className="toast "+kind;
  const ic={ok:"✓",warn:"!",err:"✕",info:"i"}[kind]||"i";
  el.innerHTML=`<span class="ic">${ic}</span><span class="jp">${msg}</span>`;
  if(action){ const b=document.createElement("button"); b.className="btn sm"; b.textContent=action.label;
    b.onclick=()=>{action.fn();kill();}; el.appendChild(b); }
  const x=document.createElement("button"); x.className="btn sm icon"; x.textContent="✕"; x.onclick=kill; el.appendChild(x);
  wrap.appendChild(el);
  const tm=setTimeout(kill,action?7000:3600);
  function kill(){ clearTimeout(tm); if(!el.isConnected)return; el.classList.add("out"); setTimeout(()=>el.remove(),240); }
  while(wrap.children.length>4)wrap.firstElementChild.remove();
  $("#stMsg").textContent=String(msg).replace(/<[^>]+>/g,"");
}

/* ---------- undo ---------- */
function snap(){ return JSON.stringify({files:S.files,activeId:S.activeId,sel:[...S.sel],pinned:S.pinned}); }
function pushUndo(){ S.undo.push(snap()); if(S.undo.length>60)S.undo.shift(); S.redo.length=0; syncUndoBtns(); }
function restoreSnap(s){ const d=JSON.parse(s); S.files=d.files; S.activeId=d.activeId; S.sel=new Set(d.sel); S.pinned=d.pinned; render(); }
function doUndo(){ if(!S.undo.length)return toast("Nothing to undo","info"); S.redo.push(snap()); restoreSnap(S.undo.pop()); syncUndoBtns(); sfx("click"); toast("Undone","ok"); }
function doRedo(){ if(!S.redo.length)return toast("Nothing to redo","info"); S.undo.push(snap()); restoreSnap(S.redo.pop()); syncUndoBtns(); sfx("click"); toast("Redone","ok"); }
function syncUndoBtns(){ $("#btnUndo").disabled=!S.undo.length; $("#btnRedo").disabled=!S.redo.length; }
function touch(f){ if(f){f.dirty=true;} }

/* ---------- NGUI compiler ---------- */
const TOK=/(\[rub:([^\]]*)\]([\s\S]*?)\[\/rub\])|(\[\/?(?:b|i|u|s|sub|sup|c|url)\])|(\[url=[^\]]*\])|(\[\/\])|(\[(?:size=)?[^\]]*\])|(<size=[^>]*>)|(<\/size>)|(\{playername\}|\{0\})|(\[[0-9a-fA-F]{6}(?:[0-9a-fA-F]{2})?(?:-[0-9a-fA-F]{6}(?:[0-9a-fA-F]{2})?)?\])|(\[-])/g;
function compileNGUI(src){
  let out="",st={b:0,i:0,u:0,s:0,sub:0,sup:0,scale:1},cols=[],last=0;
  const wrap=(txt)=>{
    if(!txt)return "";
    let styles=[],cls=[];
    if(st.b)styles.push("font-weight:800");
    if(st.i)styles.push("font-style:italic");
    const dec=[st.u?"underline":"",st.s?"line-through":""].filter(Boolean).join(" ");
    if(dec)styles.push("text-decoration:"+dec);
    if(st.sub)styles.push("vertical-align:sub;font-size:.72em");
    if(st.sup)styles.push("vertical-align:super;font-size:.72em");
    if(st.scale!==1)styles.push("font-size:"+st.scale+"em");
    const top=cols[cols.length-1];
    let inner=esc(txt).replace(/\n/g,"<br>");
    if(top){
      if(top[1]){ cls.push("grad"); styles.push(`background-image:linear-gradient(180deg,${top[0]},${top[1]})`); }
      else styles.push("color:"+top[0]);
    }
    return `<span class="${cls.join(" ")}" style="${styles.join(";")}">${inner}</span>`;
  };
  const norm=h=>"#"+h.slice(0,6);
  src.replace(TOK,(m,rub,rt,base,style,url,reset,brk,sz1,sz2,ph,hex,rst,off)=>{
    if(off>last)out+=wrap(src.slice(last,off));
    last=off+m.length;
    if(rub!==undefined){ out+=`<ruby>${wrap(base)}<rt>${esc(rt)}</rt></ruby>`; return m; }
    if(ph!==undefined){ out+=`<span class="ph">{playername}</span>`; return m; }
    if(rst!==undefined){ cols.pop(); return m; }
    if(hex!==undefined){ const p=hex.slice(1,-1).split("-"); cols.push([norm(p[0]),p[1]?norm(p[1]):null]); return m; }
    if(sz1!==undefined||sz2!==undefined){
      const raw=(sz1||sz2).replace(/[<>\[\]]/g,"").replace(/^size=/,"");
      let v=parseFloat(raw);
      if(!isNaN(v))st.scale=clamp(raw.includes("%")?v/100:v/15,.5,3);
      return m;
    }
    if(reset!==undefined){ st={b:0,i:0,u:0,s:0,sub:0,sup:0,scale:1}; return m; }
    if(style!==undefined){
      const k=style.slice(1,-1).toLowerCase();
      if(k[0]==="/"){ const n=k.slice(1); if(n in st)st[n]=0; }
      else if(k==="sub")st.sub=1; else if(k==="sup")st.sup=1; else if(k in st)st[k]=1;
      return m;
    }
    return m;
  });
  if(last<src.length)out+=wrap(src.slice(last));
  return out||`<span style="color:var(--dim)">(empty box)</span>`;
}
const TAGRE=/(\[rub:[^\]]*\][\s\S]*?\[\/rub\])|(\[\/?[a-z]+\])|(\[\/\])|(<\/?size=[^>]*>)|(\[[0-9a-fA-F]{6}(?:[0-9a-fA-F]{2})?(?:-[0-9a-fA-F]{6}(?:[0-9a-fA-F]{2})?)?\])|(\[-)|(\{playername\}|\{0\})/g;
function stripTags(t){ return t.replace(/\[rub:([^\]]*)\]([\s\S]*?)\[\/rub\]/g,"$2").replace(TAGRE,""); }
function renderedLen(t){ const s=stripTags(t).replace(/\{playername\}|\{0\}/g,"プレイヤー"); return s.length; }
function maxLineLen(t){ return stripTags(t).split("\n").reduce((m,l)=>Math.max(m,l.replace(/\{playername\}|\{0\}/g,"プレイヤー").length),0); }

/* highlight underlay, per logical line so gutter can measure wraps */
function paint(ta){
  const wrapEl=ta.closest(".edwrap"); if(!wrapEl)return;
  const pre=$(".hl",wrapEl), stack=ta.closest(".edstack"), gut=stack?$(".gutter",stack):null;
  const txt=ta.value, q=S.ui.query.trim();
  const lines=txt.split("\n");
  pre.innerHTML=lines.map(line=>{
    const marks=[];
    let m; TAGRE.lastIndex=0;
    while((m=TAGRE.exec(line))){ marks.push([m.index,m.index+m[0].length, m[0].startsWith("{")?"ph":"tag"]); }
    if(q){ const low=line.toLowerCase(),ql=q.toLowerCase(); let i=0;
      while((i=low.indexOf(ql,i))!==-1){ marks.push([i,i+q.length,"hit"]); i+=Math.max(1,q.length); } }
    marks.sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]||b[1]-a[1]);
    let out="",pos=0;
    for(const [a,b,k] of marks){ if(a<pos)continue; out+=esc(line.slice(pos,a))+`<span class="${k}">${esc(line.slice(a,b))}</span>`; pos=b; }
    out+=esc(line.slice(pos));
    return `<span class="ln">${out||"&#8203;"}</span>`;
  }).join("");
  if(gut){
    const caretLine=txt.slice(0,ta.selectionStart).split("\n").length;
    const hs=$$(".ln",pre).map(e=>e.getBoundingClientRect().height);
    gut.innerHTML=hs.map((h,i)=>`<div class="${i+1===caretLine?"cur":""}" style="height:${h}px">${i+1}</div>`).join("");
    gut.scrollTop=ta.scrollTop;
  }
  pre.scrollTop=ta.scrollTop; pre.scrollLeft=ta.scrollLeft;
  ta.style.height="auto";
  ta.style.height=Math.max(pre.scrollHeight+2, 2*parseFloat(getComputedStyle(ta).lineHeight)+28)+"px";
}

/* ---------- CSIF ---------- */
function serializeCSIF(f,withMeta=true){
  const L=[];
  if(withMeta&&(f.meta.title||f.meta.subtitle||f.meta.summary)){
    if(f.meta.title)L.push("@@!title "+f.meta.title);
    if(f.meta.subtitle)L.push("@@!subtitle "+f.meta.subtitle);
    if(f.meta.summary)L.push("@@!summary "+f.meta.summary.replace(/\n/g,"\\n"));
    L.push("");
  }
  f.boxes.forEach(b=>{ L.push(b.speaker?"@@"+b.speaker:"@@_"); L.push(b.text); L.push(""); });
  return L.join("\n").trim()+"\n";
}
function isCSIF(t){ return /^\s*@@/m.test(t||""); }
function parseCSIF(t){
  const res={meta:{title:"",subtitle:"",summary:""},blocks:[],hasMeta:false};
  const lines=String(t).replace(/\r\n?/g,"\n").split("\n");
  let cur=null;
  for(const ln of lines){
    const mm=ln.match(/^@@!(title|subtitle|summary)\s?(.*)$/);
    if(mm){ res.meta[mm[1]]=mm[2].replace(/\\n/g,"\n"); res.hasMeta=true; cur=null; continue; }
    if(/^@@#(\d+)\|(.*)$/.test(ln)){ const m=ln.match(/^@@#(\d+)\|(.*)$/); cur={speaker:m[2].trim(),text:"",sparse:+m[1]}; res.blocks.push(cur); continue; }
    if(ln.startsWith("@@")){ const sp=ln.slice(2).trim(); cur={speaker:sp==="_"?"":sp,text:""}; res.blocks.push(cur); continue; }
    if(cur){ cur.text+=(cur.text?"\n":"")+ln; }
  }
  res.blocks.forEach(b=>b.text=b.text.replace(/\s+$/,""));
  res.blocks=res.blocks.filter(b=>b.text.length||b.speaker.length);
  return res;
}

/* ---------- rendering ---------- */
function visible(f){
  let arr=f.boxes.slice();
  const q=S.ui.query.trim().toLowerCase();
  const fl=S.ui.filter;
  arr=arr.filter(b=>{
    if(fl==="locked"&&!b.locked)return false;
    if(fl==="edited"&&!b.edited)return false;
    if(fl==="narr"&&b.speaker)return false;
    if(fl==="tags"&&!TAGRE.test(b.text))return false;
    TAGRE.lastIndex=0;
    if(q&&!((b.text+" "+b.speaker).toLowerCase().includes(q)))return false;
    return true;
  });
  const s=S.ui.sort;
  if(s==="speaker")arr.sort((a,b)=>(a.speaker||"～").localeCompare(b.speaker||"～","ja"));
  else if(s==="length")arr.sort((a,b)=>renderedLen(b.text)-renderedLen(a.text));
  else if(s==="lock")arr.sort((a,b)=>(b.locked?1:0)-(a.locked?1:0));
  return arr;
}
function titleFor(f){
  const cont=f.view.continuous, pv=f.view.preview;
  const scope=cont?"Story":"Dialogue", mode=pv?"Preview":"Editor";
  return `${scope} ${mode}`;
}
function render(){
  const f=file();
  document.documentElement.dataset.theme=S.ui.theme;
  document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--ed-size",S.ui.font+"px");
  document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--ed-lh",S.ui.density==="cozy"?1.5:S.ui.density==="roomy"?2:1.75);
  document.documentElement.style.setProperty("--pad",S.ui.density==="cozy"?"10px":S.ui.density==="roomy"?"18px":"14px");
  $("#ws").classList.toggle("no-dock",!S.ui.dockOpen);
  $("#ws").classList.toggle("no-drawer",!S.ui.drawerOpen);
  $("#fontVal").textContent=S.ui.font+"px";
  $("#pGutter").classList.toggle("on",S.ui.gutter);
  $("#pRuler").classList.toggle("on",S.ui.ruler);
  $("#tipSpeed").value=S.ui.tip; $("#density").value=S.ui.density; $("#sort").value=S.ui.sort;
  $("#btnSound").textContent=S.ui.sound?"🔊":"🔇";
  $("#btnSound").classList.toggle("on",S.ui.sound);
  $("#btnTheme").textContent=S.ui.theme==="violet"?"🌗":"☀️";
  $$(".pill[data-act='scope']").forEach(p=>p.classList.toggle("on",p.dataset.v===S.ui.scope));
  $$(".pill[data-act='filter']").forEach(p=>p.classList.toggle("on",p.dataset.v===S.ui.filter));
  $("#rawpane").classList.toggle("closed",!S.ui.rawOpen);
  $("#rawHint").textContent=S.ui.rawOpen?"click to collapse":"click to expand";

  const chip=$("#fileChip");
  chip.classList.toggle("dirty",!!(f&&f.dirty));
  $(".nm",chip).textContent=f?f.name:"No project open";

  $("#tSelect").classList.toggle("on",!!f&&f.view.select);
  $("#tSelect").classList.toggle("alt",!!f&&f.view.select);
  $("#tCont").classList.toggle("on",!!f&&f.view.continuous);
  $("#tPrev").classList.toggle("on",!!f&&f.view.preview);
  $("#tPrev").classList.toggle("warn",!!f&&f.view.preview);

  const b=curBox();
  $("#vhTitle").textContent=f?titleFor(f):"No project open";
  $("#vhCrumb").textContent=f?`Story / ${f.view.continuous?"Continuous":"Page"} / ${f.view.preview?"Compiled":"Raw"}`:"Open a file to begin";
  const vb=visible(f||{boxes:[]});
  $("#vhScope").textContent=f?(f.view.continuous?`${vb.length} of ${f.boxes.length} boxes`:`Box ${curIdx()+1} of ${f.boxes.length}`):"0 boxes";
  $("#vhRO").classList.toggle("hidden",!(f&&f.view.preview));
  $("#vhLock").classList.toggle("hidden",!(b&&b.locked));
  $("#pinChip").classList.toggle("on",!!S.pinned);
  $("#pinName").textContent=S.pinned||"";

  renderDock(); renderEditor(); renderRaw(); renderInspector(); renderReference(); renderDecisions(); renderStatus(); renderSearch();
  syncUndoBtns(); persist();
}
function renderDock(){
  $("#fileCount").textContent=S.files.length;
  $("#tabProject").classList.toggle("on",S.ui.dockTab==="project");
  $("#tabSearch").classList.toggle("on",S.ui.dockTab==="search");
  $("#pbProject").hidden=S.ui.dockTab!=="project";
  $("#pbSearch").hidden=S.ui.dockTab!=="search";
  const p=$("#pbProject");
  if(!S.files.length){
    p.innerHTML=`<div class="empty" id="drop">
      <div class="orb"><i>📂</i></div>
      <h4>No opened project</h4>
      <p>Drag &amp; drop berkas <b>.csif</b> / <b>.txt</b> di sini, atau buka lewat tombol di bawah. Tab ini juga bisa diklik lagi untuk membuka file.</p>
      <button class="btn primary big" data-act="open-files">📂 Open Files…</button>
      <button class="btn" data-act="open-folder">🗂 Open Folder…</button>
      <button class="btn sm" data-act="load-sample">✦ Load sample story</button>
      <p style="font-size:11px;color:var(--dim)">Bundle <code>.unity3d</code> memerlukan aplikasi desktop (UnityPy).</p>
    </div>`;
  }else{
    p.innerHTML=`<div style="display:flex;gap:6px;margin-bottom:8px">
        <input class="mini" id="ffilter" placeholder="Filter filename…" style="flex:1 1 auto" value="">
        <button class="btn sm icon" data-act="open-files" data-tip-name="Open more files" data-tip-desc="Multi-select didukung; setiap file mempertahankan state view &amp; kuncinya sendiri." data-tip-ex="Buka 3 cerita sekaligus, lalu bandingkan istilah.">⊕</button>
      </div>
      <div id="flist">${S.files.map(f=>`
        <div class="frow ${f.id===S.activeId?"on":""}" data-act="switch-file" data-id="${f.id}">
          <span class="ic">${f.kind==="bundle"?"U3D":f.kind==="csif"?"CSIF":"TXT"}</span>
          <span class="meta"><span class="nm">${esc(f.name)} ${f.dirty?'<span class="dirtyflag">*</span>':""}</span>
          <span class="sub">${f.boxes.length} boxes · ${f.boxes.filter(x=>x.locked).length} locked</span></span>
          <button class="btn sm icon x" data-act="close-file" data-id="${f.id}" data-tip-name="Close file" data-tip-desc="Menutup dokumen. Bila ada perubahan belum disimpan, Anda ditawari Save / Discard / Cancel." data-tip-ex="Prototipe: konfirmasi sederhana + Undo.">✕</button>
        </div>`).join("")}</div>
      <div class="grouphead">Saved views</div>
      <div id="svlist">${S.views.length?S.views.map((v,i)=>`
        <div class="sv"><span class="nm" data-act="sv-apply" data-i="${i}">${esc(v.name)}</span>
        <button class="btn sm icon" data-act="sv-del" data-i="${i}" data-tip-name="Delete saved view" data-tip-desc="Menghapus preset tampilan ini." data-tip-ex="Tidak memengaruhi isi dokumen.">🗑</button></div>`).join("")
        :`<div style="font:500 11.5px/1.6 var(--ff-ui);color:var(--dim);padding:4px">Belum ada. Tekan <b>☆ Save view</b> untuk menyimpan kombinasi view + filter + sort.</div>`}</div>`;
    const ff=$("#ffilter");
    if(ff)ff.oninput=()=>{ const q=ff.value.toLowerCase();
      $$("#flist .frow").forEach(r=>{ const f=S.files.find(x=>x.id===r.dataset.id); r.style.display=f&&f.name.toLowerCase().includes(q)?"":"none"; }); };
  }
}
function editorStack(b,f,card){
  const ro=f.view.preview, locked=b.locked;
  if(ro){
    return `<div class="edstack"><div class="prev" tabindex="0" data-prev="${b.uid}" data-tip-name="Compiled preview (read-only)" data-tip-k="Alt+3 untuk keluar" data-tip-desc="Teks boleh disorot dan disalin. Mengetik tidak mengubah dokumen — gunakan tombol Edit atau Alt+3 untuk kembali ke mode Raw." data-tip-ex="Coba sorot teks ini lalu Ctrl+C — berhasil. Coba mengetik — ditolak dengan notifikasi.">
      ${compileNGUI(b.text)}
      <span class="editcue"><button class="btn sm" data-act="edit-this" data-id="${b.uid}">✎ Edit this box</button></span>
    </div></div>`;
  }
  const ruler=S.ui.ruler?`<div class="ruler">     ....5...10...15...20...25...30...35...40...45...50...55...60...65...70...75...80</div>`:"";
  return `${ruler}<div class="edstack">
    ${S.ui.gutter?`<div class="gutter" aria-hidden="true"></div>`:""}
    <div class="edwrap"><pre class="hl" aria-hidden="true"></pre>
      <textarea class="ta" spellcheck="false" data-ta="${b.uid}" ${locked?"readonly":""}
        aria-label="Box text">${esc(b.text)}</textarea></div>
  </div>
  <div class="speakerrow">
    <span class="lbl">Speaker</span>
    <input data-spk="${b.uid}" value="${esc(b.speaker)}" placeholder="(narration)" ${locked?"readonly":""}
      data-tip-name="Speaker name" data-tip-desc="Nama tokoh. Kosong berarti narasi (record tag, bukan print dengan speaker kosong)." data-tip-ex="カノン · ミツキ · ソノ · ３人">
    <button class="btn sm" data-act="lock-box" data-id="${b.uid}" data-tip-name="Toggle lock for this box" data-tip-k="Ctrl+L" data-tip-desc="Kunci per box. Terkunci: caret tetap ada, masukan ditolak, Cut menjadi Copy." data-tip-ex="Ikon gembok di header menyala saat terkunci.">${locked?"🔒 Locked":"🔓 Unlocked"}</button>
  </div>`;
}
function renderEditor(){
  const area=$("#edarea"), f=file();
  if(!f){ area.innerHTML=`<div class="empty" style="height:100%">
      <div class="orb"><i>✦</i></div><h4>Belum ada cerita dibuka</h4>
      <p>Buka berkas CSIF/teks, atau muat cerita contoh untuk mencoba seluruh keputusan B1–B10 secara langsung.</p>
      <button class="btn primary big" data-act="open-files">📂 Open Files…</button>
      <button class="btn" data-act="load-sample">✦ Load sample story</button></div>`; return; }
  const vb=visible(f);
  let head="";
  if(f.view.preview)head+=`<div class="ro-banner">👁 <span><b>READ-ONLY PREVIEW.</b> Teks bisa disorot &amp; disalin. Untuk mengubah isi, keluar dari Preview (<b>Alt+3</b>) atau tekan <b>Edit this box</b>.</span></div>`;
  if(S.ui.filter!=="all"||S.ui.query.trim())head+=`<div class="sortnote">🔎 Menampilkan <b>${vb.length}</b> dari ${f.boxes.length} box (filter <b>${S.ui.filter}</b>${S.ui.query.trim()?` · kueri “${esc(S.ui.query)}”`:""}). <button class="btn sm" data-act="clear-filter">Clear</button></div>`;
  if(S.ui.sort!=="index")head+=`<div class="sortnote">↕ Urutan tampilan ≠ urutan cerita. Drag-reorder dinonaktifkan; nomor box tetap nomor cerita asli.</div>`;
  if(f.view.select&&S.sel.size)head+=`<div class="bulkbar">☑ <b>${S.sel.size}</b> dipilih
      <button class="btn sm" data-act="bulk-lock" data-v="1">🔒 Lock all</button>
      <button class="btn sm" data-act="bulk-lock" data-v="0">🔓 Unlock all</button>
      <button class="btn sm" data-act="copy-box">📋 Copy</button>
      <button class="btn sm danger" data-act="del-box">🗑 Delete</button>
      <button class="btn sm" data-act="sel-clear">Clear selection</button></div>`;

  if(f.view.continuous){
    const meta=`<div class="card ${f.view.cur==="meta"?"active":""}">
      <div class="chead"><span class="selslot hidden"></span><span class="lockbtn" style="opacity:.2">◆</span>
        <span class="bnum">BOX #000</span><span class="spk">STORY METADATA</span>
        <span class="len">Box 0 · bukan dialog</span></div>
      <div class="metabox">
        <input data-meta="title" value="${esc(f.meta.title)}" placeholder="Episode title">
        <input data-meta="subtitle" value="${esc(f.meta.subtitle)}" placeholder="Chapter subtitle">
        <textarea data-meta="summary" placeholder="Synopsis / summary">${esc(f.meta.summary)}</textarea>
      </div></div>`;
    area.innerHTML=head+meta+vb.map(b=>{
      const i=f.boxes.indexOf(b), n=i+1, over=maxLineLen(b.text)>45;
      const dragOK=S.ui.sort==="index"&&S.ui.filter==="all"&&!S.ui.query.trim();
      return `<div class="card ${b.uid===f.view.cur?"active":""} ${b.locked?"locked":""}" data-card="${b.uid}">
        <div class="chead">
          <span class="selslot ${f.view.select?"":"hidden"}"><input type="checkbox" data-sel="${b.uid}" ${S.sel.has(b.uid)?"checked":""} aria-label="Select box ${n}"></span>
          <button class="lockbtn ${b.locked?"on":""}" data-act="lock-box" data-id="${b.uid}"
            data-tip-name="${b.locked?"Unlock box":"Lock box"} #${n}" data-tip-k="Ctrl+L"
            data-tip-desc="${b.locked?"Box ini terkunci: pengetikan, NGUI, Cut, dan Delete ditolak. Cut menjadi Copy.":"Mengunci box ini dari perubahan. Caret tetap terlihat, tetapi masukan ditolak dengan notifikasi."}"
            data-tip-ex="Setiap box punya gembok; yang terbuka sengaja redup agar tidak mencolok.">${b.locked?"🔒":"🔓"}</button>
          <span class="drag ${dragOK?"":"off"}" draggable="${dragOK}" data-drag="${b.uid}"
            data-tip-name="${dragOK?"Drag to reorder":"Reorder unavailable"}"
            data-tip-desc="${dragOK?"Tarik untuk memindahkan box dalam urutan cerita. Satu langkah undo.":"Aktif hanya bila sort = Box number dan tidak ada filter/kueri, agar perpindahan tidak ambigu."}"
            data-tip-ex="Box 7 → seret ke atas Box 3.">⠿</span>
          <span class="dot">◆</span><span class="bnum">BOX #${String(n).padStart(3,"0")}</span>
          <span class="spk ${b.speaker?"":"narr"}">${esc(b.speaker||"(narration)")}</span>
          ${b.voice?`<span class="voice">♪ ${esc(b.voice)}</span>`:""}
          <span class="len ${over?"over":""}">${renderedLen(b.text)} chars${over?" ⚠ overflow":""}</span>
        </div>
        ${editorStack(b,f,true)}
      </div>`;
    }).join("")||`<div class="empty"><h4>Tidak ada box yang cocok</h4><p>Longgarkan filter atau hapus kueri.</p><button class="btn" data-act="clear-filter">Clear filter</button></div>`;
  }else{
    const b=curBox();
    if(!b){ area.innerHTML=head+`<div class="empty"><h4>Cerita ini kosong</h4><button class="btn primary" data-act="new-box">＋ New box</button></div>`; return; }
    const n=curIdx()+1, over=maxLineLen(b.text)>45;
    area.innerHTML=head+`<div class="card active ${b.locked?"locked":""}" data-card="${b.uid}">
      <div class="chead">
        <span class="selslot ${f.view.select?"":"hidden"}"><input type="checkbox" data-sel="${b.uid}" ${S.sel.has(b.uid)?"checked":""}></span>
        <button class="lockbtn ${b.locked?"on":""}" data-act="lock-box" data-id="${b.uid}" data-tip-name="${b.locked?"Unlock":"Lock"} box #${n}" data-tip-k="Ctrl+L" data-tip-desc="Kunci per box; masukan ditolak dengan notifikasi, Cut menjadi Copy." data-tip-ex="Coba ketik saat terkunci.">${b.locked?"🔒":"🔓"}</button>
        <span class="dot">◆</span><span class="bnum">BOX #${String(n).padStart(3,"0")}</span>
        <span class="spk ${b.speaker?"":"narr"}">${esc(b.speaker||"(narration)")}</span>
        ${b.voice?`<span class="voice">♪ ${esc(b.voice)}</span>`:""}
        <span class="len ${over?"over":""}">${renderedLen(b.text)} chars · max line ${maxLineLen(b.text)}${over?" ⚠":""}</span>
      </div>
      ${editorStack(b,f,false)}
    </div>`;
  }
  $$(".ta",area).forEach(paint);
}
function renderRaw(){
  const f=file(), b=curBox(), out=$("#rawbody");
  if(!f||!b){ out.textContent="(no document)"; return; }
  const i=curIdx();
  const lines=[];
  if(f.meta.title)lines.push(`[000] title          '${f.meta.title}'`);
  if(f.meta.subtitle)lines.push(`[001] subtitle       '${f.meta.subtitle}'`);
  if(b.voice)lines.push(`<span class="act"> >> </span>[${String(i*3+2).padStart(3,"0")}] voice          '${b.voice}'`);
  if(b.speaker)lines.push(`<span class="act"> >> </span>[${String(i*3+3).padStart(3,"0")}] print          '${b.speaker}'  '${b.text.replace(/\n/g,"\\n")}'`);
  else lines.push(`<span class="act"> >> </span>[${String(i*3+3).padStart(3,"0")}] tag            '${b.text.replace(/\n/g,"\\n")}'`);
  lines.push(`    [${String(i*3+4).padStart(3,"0")}] page           ''`);
  out.innerHTML=lines.map(l=>l.startsWith("<span")||l.startsWith("[")?l:esc(l)).join("\n");
}
function renderInspector(){
  const f=file(), b=curBox(), p=$("#pbInspector");
  $("#pbInspector").hidden=S.ui.drawerTab!=="inspector";
  $("#pbReference").hidden=S.ui.drawerTab!=="reference";
  $("#pbDecisions").hidden=S.ui.drawerTab!=="decisions";
  $$(".tab[data-act='drawer-tab']").forEach(t=>t.classList.toggle("on",t.dataset.v===S.ui.drawerTab));
  if(!f||!b){ p.innerHTML=`<div class="note"><h5>Inspector</h5><p>Buka cerita dan pilih box untuk melihat detail record, panjang render, dan status kunci.</p></div>`; return; }
  const segs=b.text.split("\n");
  p.innerHTML=`<div class="insp">
    <div class="kv"><span>Box</span><span>#${curIdx()+1} / ${f.boxes.length}</span></div>
    <div class="kv"><span>Record uid</span><span>${b.uid}</span></div>
    <div class="kv"><span>Kind</span><span>${b.speaker?"print":"tag (narration)"}</span></div>
    <div class="kv"><span>Speaker</span><span>${esc(b.speaker||"—")}</span></div>
    <div class="kv"><span>Voice cue</span><span>${esc(b.voice||"—")}</span></div>
    <div class="kv"><span>Lock</span><span style="color:${b.locked?"var(--crimson)":"var(--mint)"}">${b.locked?"LOCKED":"open"}</span></div>
    <div class="kv"><span>Rendered chars</span><span>${renderedLen(b.text)}</span></div>
    <div class="kv"><span>Max line</span><span style="color:${maxLineLen(b.text)>45?"var(--magenta)":"inherit"}">${maxLineLen(b.text)} / 45</span></div>
    <div class="kv"><span>Logical lines</span><span>${segs.length}</span></div>
    <div class="kv"><span>Edited</span><span>${b.edited?"yes":"no"}</span></div>
    <div class="grouphead">Segments (raw)</div>
    ${segs.map((s,i)=>`<div class="seg"><b>SEG ${i+1}</b>${esc(s)||"<i style='color:var(--dim)'>(empty)</i>"}</div>`).join("")}
    <div class="grouphead">Compiled</div>
    <div class="prev" style="min-height:auto">${compileNGUI(b.text)}</div>
  </div>`;
}
function renderReference(){
  $("#pbReference").innerHTML=`<div style="font:500 11.5px/1.6 var(--ff-ui);color:var(--muted);margin-bottom:9px">Klik untuk menyisipkan/membungkus seleksi di editor aktif. Semua pola bersumber dari satu definisi NGUI.</div>
    <div class="gloss">${GLOSSARY.map((g,i)=>`<div class="gitem" data-act="gloss" data-i="${i}">
      <code>${esc(g.code)}</code><div class="t">${esc(g.t)}</div><div class="d">${esc(g.d)}</div></div>`).join("")}</div>`;
}
function renderDecisions(){
  $("#pbDecisions").innerHTML=`<div class="note" style="border-color:var(--violet)">
      <h5>📝 Jawaban atas temuan Anda</h5>
      <p>Setiap butir di bawah <b>sudah dijalankan</b> di prototipe ini — bukan sekadar rencana. Silakan uji langsung, lalu bandingkan dengan vonisnya.</p>
    </div>`+DECISIONS.map(d=>`<div class="note">
      <h5><span class="verd ${d.v}">${d.v==="bug"?"bug · setuju":d.v==="idea"?"ide · setuju":"setuju + revisi"}</span> ${esc(d.id)} — ${esc(d.t)}</h5>
      ${d.p.map(p=>`<p>${p}</p>`).join("")}
    </div>`).join("");
}
function renderStatus(){
  const f=file(), b=curBox();
  $("#stFile").innerHTML=f?`📄 <b>${esc(f.name)}</b>${f.dirty?" *":""}`:"📄 no file";
  $("#stBox").innerHTML=f&&b?`◆ Box <b>${curIdx()+1}</b>/${f.boxes.length}`:"◆ —";
  $("#stChars").innerHTML=b?`✎ <b>${renderedLen(b.text)}</b> rendered · ${b.text.split("\n").length} lines`:"✎ —";
  $("#stSel").innerHTML=S.sel.size?`☑ <b>${S.sel.size}</b> selected`:(S.pinned?`📌 <b>${esc(S.pinned)}</b>`:"☑ none");
  const c=f?f.boxes:[];
  $("#cAll").textContent=c.length; $("#cLock").textContent=c.filter(x=>x.locked).length;
  $("#cEdit").textContent=c.filter(x=>x.edited).length; $("#cNarr").textContent=c.filter(x=>!x.speaker).length;
  $("#cTags").textContent=c.filter(x=>{TAGRE.lastIndex=0;return TAGRE.test(x.text)}).length;
}
function renderSearch(){
  const q=S.ui.query.trim(), p=$("#pbSearch");
  if(!q){ p.innerHTML=`<div class="note"><h5>🔎 Search</h5><p>Ketik kueri di atas untuk mencari dialog, speaker, atau tag. Hasil muncul di sini dan disorot di editor.</p><p style="color:var(--dim)">Contoh: <code>鐘</code> <code>カノン</code> <code>[b]</code> <code>{playername}</code></p></div>`; $("#resCount").textContent=0; return; }
  const files=S.ui.scope==="all"?S.files:[file()].filter(Boolean);
  const ql=q.toLowerCase(); let total=0, html="";
  files.forEach(f=>{
    const hits=[];
    f.boxes.forEach((b,i)=>{
      const hay=b.text+"\n"+b.speaker, low=hay.toLowerCase();
      let k=low.indexOf(ql);
      while(k!==-1){ hits.push({b,i,k}); k=low.indexOf(ql,k+Math.max(1,q.length)); }
    });
    const mt=["title","subtitle","summary"].filter(k=>f.meta[k].toLowerCase().includes(ql));
    if(!hits.length&&!mt.length)return;
    html+=`<div class="grouphead">${esc(f.name)} · ${hits.length} hits${mt.length?" · meta":""}</div>`;
    mt.forEach(k=>{ total++; html+=`<div class="res" data-act="jump" data-fid="${f.id}" data-bid="meta"><div class="top">◆ BOX 000 · ${k}</div><div class="snip">${mark(esc(f.meta[k]),q)}</div></div>`; });
    hits.slice(0,80).forEach(h=>{ total++;
      const s=Math.max(0,h.k-24), e=Math.min(h.b.text.length,h.k+q.length+34);
      html+=`<div class="res" data-act="jump" data-fid="${f.id}" data-bid="${h.b.uid}">
        <div class="top">◆ BOX ${String(h.i+1).padStart(3,"0")} · ${esc(h.b.speaker||"narration")}${h.b.locked?" 🔒":""}</div>
        <div class="snip">${mark(esc(h.b.text.slice(s,e).replace(/\n/g," ⏎ ")),q)}</div></div>`;
    });
  });
  p.innerHTML=html||`<div class="note"><h5>Tidak ada kecocokan</h5><p>Tidak ditemukan “${esc(q)}” pada scope <b>${S.ui.scope==="all"?"semua file":"file ini"}</b>.</p></div>`;
  $("#resCount").textContent=total;
}
function mark(escaped,q){
  const re=new RegExp("("+q.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,"\\$&")+")","gi");
  return escaped.replace(re,"<mark>$1</mark>");
}

/* ---------- mutations ---------- */
function setCur(id){ const f=file(); if(!f)return; f.view.cur=id; }
function activeTextarea(){
  const f=file(); if(!f)return null;
  const el=document.activeElement;
  if(el&&el.classList&&el.classList.contains("ta"))return el;
  const b=curBox(); return b?$(`.ta[data-ta="${b.uid}"]`):null;
}
function targetBox(){
  const ta=activeTextarea(); const f=file(); if(!f)return null;
  if(ta)return box(f,ta.dataset.ta);
  return curBox();
}
function denyLocked(b,what){
  if(!b||!b.locked)return false;
  const f=file(), n=f?f.boxes.indexOf(b)+1:0;
  toast(`Box #${n} terkunci — buka gembok untuk ${what}.`,"warn",{label:"🔓 Unlock",fn:()=>{pushUndo();b.locked=false;sfx("unlock");render();toast(`Box #${n} unlocked`,"ok");}});
  sfx("err");
  const card=$(`[data-card="${b.uid}"]`); if(card){card.classList.add("shake");setTimeout(()=>card.classList.remove("shake"),360);}
  const pv=$(`[data-prev="${b.uid}"]`); if(pv){pv.classList.add("nudge");setTimeout(()=>pv.classList.remove("nudge"),360);}
  return true;
}
function commitText(b,val){
  const f=file(); if(!b)return;
  if(b.text===val)return;
  const now=Date.now();
  if(now-S.lastEdit>700)pushUndo();
  S.lastEdit=now;
  b.text=val; b.edited=true; touch(f);
  const card=$(`[data-card="${b.uid}"]`);
  if(card){ const len=$(".len",card); if(len){ const over=maxLineLen(b.text)>45;
    len.textContent=`${renderedLen(b.text)} chars${f.view.continuous?(over?" ⚠ overflow":""):` · max line ${maxLineLen(b.text)}${over?" ⚠":""}`}`;
    len.classList.toggle("over",over); } }
  renderRaw(); renderStatus(); renderSearch();
  const chip=$("#fileChip"); chip.classList.add("dirty");
}
function wrapSel(o,c){
  const f=file(); if(!f)return;
  if(f.view.preview){ toast("Preview bersifat read-only — keluar dulu (Alt+3).","warn",{label:"✎ Exit preview",fn:()=>{f.view.preview=false;render();}}); sfx("err"); return; }
  const b=targetBox(); if(!b)return;
  if(denyLocked(b,"menerapkan NGUI"))return;
  const ta=$(`.ta[data-ta="${b.uid}"]`); if(!ta)return;
  pushUndo();
  const s=ta.selectionStart,e=ta.selectionEnd, sel=ta.value.slice(s,e);
  const nv=ta.value.slice(0,s)+o+sel+c+ta.value.slice(e);
  ta.value=nv; commitText(b,nv);
  ta.focus(); const caret=s+o.length+sel.length; ta.setSelectionRange(caret,caret);
  paint(ta); renderInspector(); sfx("click");
}
function newBox(){
  const f=file(); if(!f)return toast("Buka cerita dulu","warn");
  pushUndo();
  const i=Math.max(0,curIdx()), b={uid:uid(),speaker:"",voice:"",text:"",locked:false,edited:true};
  f.boxes.splice(i+1,0,b); f.view.cur=b.uid; touch(f); render(); sfx("apply");
  toast(`Box baru disisipkan setelah #${i+1} → kini #${i+2}`,"ok");
  const ta=$(`.ta[data-ta="${b.uid}"]`); if(ta){ta.focus();ta.scrollIntoView({block:"center",behavior:"smooth"});}
}
function targets(){
  const f=file(); if(!f)return[];
  if(S.sel.size)return f.boxes.filter(b=>S.sel.has(b.uid));
  const b=curBox(); return b?[b]:[];
}
function copyBoxes(){
  const t=targets(); if(!t.length)return;
  const f=file();
  const txt=t.map(b=>(b.speaker?"@@"+b.speaker:"@@_")+"\n"+b.text).join("\n\n")+"\n";
  writeClip(txt); toast(`${t.length} box disalin sebagai CSIF`,"ok"); sfx("click");
}
function cutBoxes(){
  const t=targets(); if(!t.length)return;
  const locked=t.filter(b=>b.locked), free=t.filter(b=>!b.locked);
  copyBoxes();
  if(!free.length){ toast("Semua box terpilih terkunci — Cut menjadi Copy, tidak ada yang dihapus.","warn"); return; }
  pushUndo();
  const f=file(); const names=free.map(b=>f.boxes.indexOf(b)+1);
  f.boxes=f.boxes.filter(b=>!free.includes(b));
  free.forEach(b=>S.sel.delete(b.uid));
  f.view.cur=f.boxes[Math.min(f.boxes.length-1,Math.max(0,names[0]-1))]?.uid||null;
  touch(f); render(); sfx("apply");
  toast(`${free.length} box dipotong${locked.length?` · ${locked.length} terkunci hanya disalin`:""}`,"ok",{label:"Undo",fn:doUndo});
}
function delBoxes(){
  const t=targets(); if(!t.length)return;
  const free=t.filter(b=>!b.locked), locked=t.filter(b=>b.locked);
  if(!free.length){ toast("Semua box terpilih terkunci — tidak ada yang dihapus.","warn"); sfx("err"); return; }
  pushUndo();
  const f=file();
  f.boxes=f.boxes.filter(b=>!free.includes(b));
  free.forEach(b=>S.sel.delete(b.uid));
  f.view.cur=f.boxes[Math.min(f.boxes.length-1,Math.max(0,curIdx()))]?.uid||f.boxes[0]?.uid||null;
  touch(f); render(); sfx("apply");
  toast(`${free.length} box dihapus${locked.length?` · ${locked.length} terkunci dipertahankan`:""}`,"ok",{label:"Undo",fn:doUndo});
}
function toggleLock(id){
  const f=file(); if(!f)return;
  const b=id?box(f,id):curBox(); if(!b)return;
  pushUndo(); b.locked=!b.locked; touch(f); render();
  sfx(b.locked?"lock":"unlock");
  toast(`Box #${f.boxes.indexOf(b)+1} ${b.locked?"terkunci — masukan akan ditolak":"dibuka"}`,b.locked?"warn":"ok");
}
function bulkLock(on){
  const f=file(); if(!f||!S.sel.size)return;
  pushUndo(); let n=0;
  f.boxes.forEach(b=>{ if(S.sel.has(b.uid)){b.locked=!!on;n++;} });
  touch(f); render(); sfx(on?"lock":"unlock"); toast(`${n} box ${on?"dikunci":"dibuka"}`,"ok",{label:"Undo",fn:doUndo});
}
function jump(dir){
  const f=file(); if(!f||!f.boxes.length)return;
  const list=visible(f);
  if(!list.length)return toast("Tidak ada box pada filter ini","warn");
  const b=curBox(); let i=list.indexOf(b); if(i<0)i=0;
  if(S.pinned){
    let j=i+dir, skipped=0;
    while(j>=0&&j<list.length){ if((list[j].speaker||"")===S.pinned)break; skipped++; j+=dir; }
    if(j<0||j>=list.length){ toast(`📌 ${S.pinned} — tidak ada box lain milik speaker ini pada arah itu.`,"info"); sfx("err"); return; }
    const from=f.boxes.indexOf(b)+1, to=f.boxes.indexOf(list[j])+1;
    setCur(list[j].uid); render(); sfx("jump");
    toast(`📌 ${S.pinned} · Box ${from} → Box ${to}${skipped?` (melewati ${skipped})`:""}`,"info");
  }else{
    const j=clamp(i+dir,0,list.length-1);
    if(j===i){ toast(dir>0?"Sudah di box terakhir":"Sudah di box pertama","info"); return; }
    setCur(list[j].uid); render(); sfx("jump");
    toast(`Box ${f.boxes.indexOf(list[j])+1} / ${f.boxes.length}`,"info");
  }
  const el=$(`[data-card="${file().view.cur}"]`); if(el)el.scrollIntoView({block:"center",behavior:"smooth"});
}
function pinSpeaker(){
  const b=curBox(); if(!b)return;
  if(S.pinned){ S.pinned=null; render(); sfx("click"); toast("Pin speaker dilepas — navigasi kembali berurutan","info"); return; }
  if(!b.speaker){ toast("Box narasi tidak punya speaker untuk dipin","warn"); sfx("err"); return; }
  S.pinned=b.speaker; render(); sfx("toggle");
  toast(`📌 Navigasi dipin ke ${b.speaker} — Prev/Next melompat antar box speaker ini`,"ok");
}
function writeClip(t){
  if(navigator.clipboard&&navigator.clipboard.writeText)navigator.clipboard.writeText(t).catch(()=>fallbackCopy(t));
  else fallbackCopy(t);
}
function fallbackCopy(t){
  const ta=document.createElement("textarea"); ta.value=t; ta.style.position="fixed"; ta.style.opacity="0";
  document.body.appendChild(ta); ta.select(); try{document.execCommand("copy");}catch(e){} ta.remove();
}

/* ---------- modals ---------- */
let modalKey=null;
function modal(html,cls=""){
  closeModal();
  const root=$("#modalRoot");
  root.innerHTML=`<div class="scrim" data-scrim><div class="modal ${cls}" role="dialog" aria-modal="true">${html}</div></div>`;
  modalKey=e=>{ if(e.key==="Escape"){e.preventDefault();closeModal();} };
  document.addEventListener("keydown",modalKey);
  const first=$(".modal button,.modal input,.modal select"); if(first)first.focus();
  return $(".modal");
}
function closeModal(){
  $("#modalRoot").innerHTML=""; $("#popRoot").innerHTML="";
  if(modalKey){document.removeEventListener("keydown",modalKey);modalKey=null;}
}
function pasteChooser(text){
  const f=file(); if(!f){toast("Buka cerita dulu","warn");return;}
  const p=parseCSIF(text), n=p.blocks.length;
  if(p.hasMeta){ importPreview(text,true); return; }
  if(!n){ toast("Clipboard tidak memuat blok CSIF yang bisa diterapkan","warn"); return; }
  const i=Math.max(0,curIdx()), curNo=i+1;
  const endNo=Math.min(f.boxes.length,i+n);
  const over=Math.max(0,n-(f.boxes.length-i));
  const replaceLabel=n===1?`Replace current box (#${curNo})`:`Replace current box onward (#${curNo}–#${endNo})`;
  const replaceDesc=n===1
    ? `Menimpa teks Box <b>#${curNo}</b> saja. Speaker dapat dipertahankan atau ikut diganti.`
    : `Menimpa <b>${Math.min(n,f.boxes.length-i)}</b> box mulai <b>#${curNo}</b> sampai <b>#${endNo}</b>${over?` · <b>${over}</b> blok kelebihan akan dilewati`:""}. Pratinjau baris-per-baris muncul sebelum diterapkan.`;
  modal(`<div class="mhead">
      <span style="font-size:20px">📎</span>
      <div><h3>CSIF script detected in clipboard</h3>
      <div class="sub">${n} dialogue block${n>1?"s":""} · ${p.hasMeta?"metadata present":"no metadata"} · target starts at Box #${curNo}</div></div>
      <span class="spacer"></span><button class="btn sm icon" data-act="modal-close">✕</button>
    </div>
    <div class="mbody">
      <div class="warnbox">ℹ <span>Clipboard berisi header CSIF (<code>@@Speaker</code> / <code>@@_</code>). Satu payload bisa berarti tiga tindakan berbeda, jadi targetnya harus Anda pilih eksplisit — tidak ada yang diterapkan diam-diam.</span></div>
      <button class="opt" data-act="pc-insert"><span class="oi">📥</span><span><span class="ot">Insert as new boxes — after Box #${curNo}</span><span class="od">${n} box baru disisipkan mulai <b>#${curNo+1}</b>. Box yang ada tidak berubah; seluruhnya jadi satu langkah Undo.</span></span></button>
      <button class="opt hi" data-act="pc-replace"><span class="oi">🔄</span><span><span class="ot">${replaceLabel}</span><span class="od">${replaceDesc}</span></span></button>
      <button class="opt" data-act="pc-text"><span class="oi">📝</span><span><span class="ot">Paste as plain text at caret</span><span class="od">Menyisipkan isi clipboard apa adanya ke editor aktif. Header <code>@@</code> akan ikut sebagai teks — jarang diinginkan.</span></span></button>
      <div style="font:600 11px/1.6 var(--ff-mono);color:var(--dim);background:var(--ink-850);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:10px;padding:9px;max-height:120px;overflow:auto;white-space:pre-wrap">${esc(text.slice(0,600))}${text.length>600?"…":""}</div>
    </div>
    <div class="mfoot"><span style="font:600 11px/1.5 var(--ff-ui);color:var(--dim)">Payload dengan metadata Box 0 langsung membuka Import Preview.</span><span class="spacer"></span><button class="btn" data-act="modal-close">Cancel</button></div>`,"sm");
  $("#modalRoot").dataset.payload=text;
}
function importPreview(text,fromMeta){
  const f=file(); if(!f)return;
  const p=parseCSIF(text);
  const start=Math.max(0,curIdx());
  const count=Math.min(p.blocks.length,Math.max(0,f.boxes.length-start));
  const m=modal(`<div class="mhead">
      <span style="font-size:20px">🔍</span>
      <div><h3>Import preview — before / after</h3>
      <div class="sub">${p.blocks.length} blocks · target Box #${start+1}–#${start+count} of ${f.boxes.length}${fromMeta?" · metadata payload":""}</div></div>
      <span class="spacer"></span><button class="btn sm icon" data-act="modal-close">✕</button>
    </div>
    <div class="mbody">
      ${p.blocks.length>f.boxes.length-start?`<div class="warnbox">⚠ <span><b>${p.blocks.length-(f.boxes.length-start)}</b> blok melebihi sisa box dan akan dilewati. Gunakan Insert bila maksudnya menambah box.</span></div>`:""}
      ${count===0?`<div class="warnbox">⚠ <span><b>0 applicable rows.</b> Tidak ada box tersisa dari posisi ini. Tutup dialog lalu pilih box lebih awal, atau gunakan Insert as new boxes.</span></div>`:""}
      <div style="display:flex;gap:16px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:11px">
        <label class="chk"><input type="checkbox" id="apSpk" ${p.blocks.some(b=>b.speaker)?"checked":""}> Apply speaker names</label>
        ${p.hasMeta?`<label class="chk"><input type="checkbox" id="apMeta" checked> Apply metadata to Box 0</label>`:""}
        <span class="spacer"></span>
        <span style="font:600 11px/1 var(--ff-mono);color:var(--dim)" id="apStat"></span>
      </div>
      ${p.hasMeta?`<div class="note" style="margin-bottom:11px"><h5>Box 0 metadata</h5>
        <p><b>Title:</b> ${esc(p.meta.title)||"—"} · <b>Subtitle:</b> ${esc(p.meta.subtitle)||"—"}</p>
        <p><b>Summary:</b> ${esc(p.meta.summary.slice(0,160))||"—"}</p></div>`:""}
      <div class="tblwrap"><table class="tbl"><thead><tr><th>Box</th><th>Current speaker</th><th>Incoming</th><th>Current text</th><th>Incoming text</th></tr></thead><tbody id="apBody"></tbody></table></div>
    </div>
    <div class="mfoot">
      <span style="font:600 11px/1.5 var(--ff-ui);color:var(--dim)">Box terkunci dilewati dan dilaporkan.</span>
      <span class="spacer"></span>
      <button class="btn" data-act="modal-close">Cancel</button>
      <button class="btn primary" data-act="ap-apply" ${count===0?"disabled":""}>✓ Apply ${count} box${count===1?"":"es"}</button>
    </div>`);
  const draw=()=>{
    const ap=$("#apSpk").checked, body=$("#apBody");
    let skip=0;
    body.innerHTML=Array.from({length:count},(_,k)=>{
      const b=f.boxes[start+k], nb=p.blocks[k], locked=b.locked;
      if(locked)skip++;
      const sp=ap&&nb.speaker?nb.speaker:b.speaker;
      return `<tr class="${locked?"skip":""}">
        <td class="n">#${start+k+1}${locked?" 🔒":""}</td>
        <td class="old">${esc(b.speaker||"(narration)")}</td>
        <td class="new">${esc(sp||"(narration)")}${locked?" <i>(kept)</i>":""}</td>
        <td class="old">${esc(b.text.replace(/\n/g," ⏎ ").slice(0,90))||"—"}</td>
        <td class="new">${locked?"<i>skipped — locked</i>":esc(nb.text.replace(/\n/g," ⏎ ").slice(0,90))||"—"}</td></tr>`;
    }).join("")||`<tr><td colspan="5" style="color:var(--dim)">Tidak ada baris yang bisa diterapkan.</td></tr>`;
    $("#apStat").textContent=`${count-skip} apply · ${skip} skipped (locked)`;
  };
  $("#apSpk").onchange=draw; draw();
  m.dataset.payload=text; m.dataset.start=start; m.dataset.count=count;
}
function applyImport(){
  const m=$(".modal"); if(!m)return;
  const f=file(), p=parseCSIF(m.dataset.payload), start=+m.dataset.start, count=+m.dataset.count;
  const ap=$("#apSpk")?.checked, am=$("#apMeta")?.checked;
  pushUndo();
  let applied=0,skipped=0;
  for(let k=0;k<count;k++){
    const b=f.boxes[start+k], nb=p.blocks[k];
    if(b.locked){skipped++;continue;}
    b.text=nb.text; if(ap&&nb.speaker)b.speaker=nb.speaker; b.edited=true; applied++;
  }
  if(am&&p.hasMeta){ f.meta.title=p.meta.title||f.meta.title; f.meta.subtitle=p.meta.subtitle||f.meta.subtitle; f.meta.summary=p.meta.summary||f.meta.summary; }
  touch(f); closeModal(); render(); sfx("apply");
  toast(`${applied} box diterapkan${skipped?` · ${skipped} terkunci dilewati`:""}${am&&p.hasMeta?" · metadata Box 0 diperbarui":""}`,"ok",{label:"Undo",fn:doUndo});
}
function insertPayload(){
  const m=$(".modal")||$("#modalRoot"); const text=m.dataset.payload||$("#modalRoot").dataset.payload;
  const f=file(), p=parseCSIF(text);
  if(!p.blocks.length)return toast("Tidak ada blok untuk disisipkan","warn");
  pushUndo();
  const i=Math.max(0,curIdx());
  const news=p.blocks.map(b=>({uid:uid(),speaker:b.speaker,voice:"",text:b.text,locked:false,edited:true}));
  f.boxes.splice(i+1,0,...news);
  if(p.hasMeta){ f.meta.title=p.meta.title||f.meta.title; f.meta.subtitle=p.meta.subtitle||f.meta.subtitle; f.meta.summary=p.meta.summary||f.meta.summary; }
  f.view.cur=news[0].uid; touch(f); closeModal(); render(); sfx("apply");
  toast(`${news.length} box disisipkan setelah #${i+1} → #${i+2}–#${i+1+news.length}`,"ok",{label:"Undo",fn:doUndo});
}
function rubyDialog(){
  const f=file(); if(!f)return;
  if(f.view.preview)return toast("Preview read-only — keluar dulu (Alt+3)","warn");
  const b=targetBox(); if(!b)return; if(denyLocked(b,"ruby"))return;
  const ta=$(`.ta[data-ta="${b.uid}"]`); const sel=ta?ta.value.slice(ta.selectionStart,ta.selectionEnd):"";
  modal(`<div class="mhead"><span style="font-size:19px">🈂</span><div><h3>Insert ruby / furigana</h3><div class="sub">[rub:reading]base[/rub]</div></div><span class="spacer"></span><button class="btn sm icon" data-act="modal-close">✕</button></div>
    <div class="mbody">
      <div class="uctl" style="margin-bottom:9px"><span class="lbl" style="min-width:70px">Base</span><input class="mini" id="rbBase" style="flex:1 1 auto;height:32px;font-family:var(--ff-jp)" value="${esc(sel)}" placeholder="願い"></div>
      <div class="uctl" style="margin-bottom:12px"><span class="lbl" style="min-width:70px">Reading</span><input class="mini" id="rbRead" style="flex:1 1 auto;height:32px;font-family:var(--ff-jp)" value="" placeholder="めいれい"></div>
      <div class="lbl" style="margin-bottom:6px">Live preview</div>
      <div class="prev" id="rbPrev" style="min-height:auto"></div>
      <div style="font:600 11px/1.6 var(--ff-mono);color:var(--dim);margin-top:9px" id="rbCode"></div>
    </div>
    <div class="mfoot"><span class="spacer"></span><button class="btn" data-act="modal-close">Cancel</button><button class="btn primary" data-act="rb-apply">✓ Insert</button></div>`,"sm");
  const upd=()=>{ const base=$("#rbBase").value,rd=$("#rbRead").value;
    const code=`[rub:${rd}]${base}[/rub]`;
    $("#rbPrev").innerHTML=compileNGUI(code); $("#rbCode").textContent=code; };
  $("#rbBase").oninput=upd; $("#rbRead").oninput=upd; upd();
  (sel?$("#rbRead"):$("#rbBase")).focus();
}
function colorPop(anchor){
  const r=anchor.getBoundingClientRect();
  $("#popRoot").innerHTML=`<div class="pop" style="left:${clamp(r.left,8,innerWidth-260)}px;top:${r.bottom+8}px;width:246px">
    <div class="lbl">Colour / vertical gradient</div>
    <div class="row"><input type="color" id="c1" value="#7b42bc"><span style="font:600 11px/1 var(--ff-mono);color:var(--muted)">top</span>
      <input type="color" id="c2" value="#ff7da7"><span style="font:600 11px/1 var(--ff-mono);color:var(--muted)">bottom</span></div>
    <div class="gradprev" id="gp">GRADIENT SAMPLE</div>
    <label class="chk"><input type="checkbox" id="gOn" checked> Use gradient (hyphen, not comma)</label>
    <div class="row"><button class="btn sm primary" data-act="c-apply" style="flex:1">✓ Insert tag</button><button class="btn sm" data-act="pop-close">✕</button></div>
  </div>`;
  const upd=()=>{ const a=$("#c1").value,b=$("#c2").value,on=$("#gOn").checked;
    const gp=$("#gp"); gp.style.backgroundImage=on?`linear-gradient(180deg,${a},${b})`:"none";
    gp.style.color=on?"transparent":a; gp.style.webkitTextFillColor=on?"transparent":a; };
  ["c1","c2","gOn"].forEach(id=>$("#"+id).oninput=upd); upd();
}

/* ---------- files ---------- */
function addFile(doc){
  S.files.push(doc); S.activeId=doc.id;
  doc.view.cur=doc.boxes[0]?.uid||null;
  S.sel.clear(); S.pinned=null; S.ui.dockTab="project";
  render();
}
function loadSample(){ const d=seedStory(); addFile(d); toast(`Sample dimuat: ${d.boxes.length} box`,"ok"); sfx("apply"); }
function openFiles(){ $("#fileInput").click(); }
function handleFiles(list){
  const arr=[...list]; if(!arr.length)return;
  let ok=0,bundles=0,fail=0;
  arr.forEach(fl=>{
    if(/\.(unity3d|asset)$/i.test(fl.name)){bundles++;return;}
    const rd=new FileReader();
    rd.onload=()=>{
      const t=String(rd.result||"");
      try{
        if(isCSIF(t)){ const p=parseCSIF(t);
          addFile({id:uid(),name:fl.name,kind:"csif",dirty:false,meta:p.meta,
            view:{continuous:true,preview:false,select:false,cur:null},
            boxes:p.blocks.map(b=>({uid:uid(),speaker:b.speaker,voice:"",text:b.text,locked:false,edited:false}))});
        }else{
          const paras=t.replace(/\r\n?/g,"\n").split(/\n{2,}/).map(s=>s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
          addFile({id:uid(),name:fl.name,kind:"text",dirty:false,meta:{title:fl.name,subtitle:"",summary:""},
            view:{continuous:true,preview:false,select:false,cur:null},
            boxes:(paras.length?paras:[t]).map(s=>({uid:uid(),speaker:"",voice:"",text:s,locked:false,edited:false}))});
        }
        ok++; sfx("apply");
        toast(`${fl.name} dibuka (${ok}/${arr.length-bundles})`,"ok");
      }catch(e){ fail++; toast(`Gagal membaca ${fl.name}`,"err"); }
    };
    rd.onerror=()=>{fail++;toast(`Gagal membaca ${fl.name}`,"err");};
    rd.readAsText(fl);
  });
  if(bundles)toast(`${bundles} bundle .unity3d dilewati — prototipe UI tidak memuat UnityPy. Gunakan aplikasi desktop untuk bundle.`,"warn");
}
function closeFile(id){
  const f=S.files.find(x=>x.id===id); if(!f)return;
  const doIt=()=>{ pushUndo(); S.files=S.files.filter(x=>x.id!==id);
    if(S.activeId===id)S.activeId=S.files[0]?.id||null;
    S.sel.clear(); S.pinned=null; render(); toast(`${f.name} ditutup`,"ok",{label:"Undo",fn:doUndo}); };
  if(f.dirty){
    modal(`<div class="mhead"><span style="font-size:19px">⚠</span><div><h3>Unsaved changes</h3><div class="sub">${esc(f.name)}</div></div></div>
      <div class="mbody"><p style="font:500 12.5px/1.7 var(--ff-ui);color:var(--muted);margin:0">Dokumen ini punya perubahan yang belum disimpan. Menutupnya akan membuang salinan di memori.</p></div>
      <div class="mfoot"><button class="btn" data-act="modal-close">Cancel</button><span class="spacer"></span>
      <button class="btn danger" data-act="cf-discard">Discard &amp; close</button>
      <button class="btn primary" data-act="cf-save" data-id="${id}">💾 Save &amp; close</button></div>`,"sm");
    $("#modalRoot").dataset.closeId=id;
  }else doIt();
}
function saveActive(){
  const f=file(); if(!f)return toast("Tidak ada dokumen aktif","warn");
  f.dirty=false; render(); sfx("apply"); toast(`${f.name} ditandai bersih (desktop: tulis atomik .tmp → verifikasi → replace)`,"ok");
}
function exportCSIF(){
  const f=file(); if(!f)return toast("Tidak ada dokumen aktif","warn");
  const blob=new Blob([serializeCSIF(f)],{type:"text/plain;charset=utf-8"});
  const a=document.createElement("a"); a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  a.download=f.name.replace(/\.(unity3d|asset)$/i,"")+".csif.txt"; a.click();
  setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href),1500);
  sfx("apply"); toast("CSIF diunduh sesuai state saat ini","ok");
}

/* ---------- tooltip (B5) ---------- */
const TIP={el:$("#tip"),t1:null,t2:null,t3:null,cur:null};
const SPEED={fast:[200,700,1400],normal:[350,1100,2200],slow:[500,1600,3200]};
function tipHide(){ clearTimeout(TIP.t1);clearTimeout(TIP.t2);clearTimeout(TIP.t3);
  TIP.el.classList.remove("show","tier2","tier3"); TIP.cur=null; }
function tipShow(target){
  if(S.ui.tip==="off")return;
  const name=target.dataset.tipName; if(!name)return;
  if(TIP.cur===target)return;
  tipHide(); TIP.cur=target;
  const [d1,d2,d3]=SPEED[S.ui.tip]||SPEED.normal;
  const k=target.dataset.tipK, d=target.dataset.tipDesc, ex=target.dataset.tipEx;
  TIP.el.innerHTML=`<div class="t1"><b>${esc(name)}</b>${k?`<span class="k">${esc(k)}</span>`:""}</div>
    ${d?`<div class="t2"><p>${esc(d)}</p></div>`:""}
    ${ex?`<div class="t3"><div class="lb">${/[→=·\[\{]/.test(ex)?"Contoh":"Untuk pemula"}</div><div class="ex">${esc(ex)}</div></div>`:""}`;
  const r=target.getBoundingClientRect();
  TIP.el.style.left="0px"; TIP.el.style.top="0px"; TIP.el.classList.add("show");
  const tw=TIP.el.offsetWidth, th=TIP.el.offsetHeight;
  let x=r.left, y=r.bottom+8;
  if(y+th>innerHeight-8)y=Math.max(8,r.top-th-8);
  if(x+tw>innerWidth-8)x=Math.max(8,innerWidth-tw-8);
  TIP.el.style.left=x+"px"; TIP.el.style.top=y+"px";
  if(d)TIP.t2=setTimeout(()=>TIP.el.classList.add("tier2"),d2-d1);
  if(ex)TIP.t3=setTimeout(()=>TIP.el.classList.add("tier3"),d3-d1);
}
document.addEventListener("pointerover",e=>{ const t=e.target.closest?.("[data-tip-name]"); if(t)tipShow(t); });
document.addEventListener("pointerout",e=>{ const t=e.target.closest?.("[data-tip-name]"); if(t&&TIP.cur===t)tipHide(); });
document.addEventListener("focusin",e=>{ const t=e.target.closest?.("[data-tip-name]"); if(t)tipShow(t); });
document.addEventListener("focusout",tipHide);
document.addEventListener("scroll",tipHide,true);
document.addEventListener("pointerdown",tipHide);

/* ---------- clipboard paste flow ---------- */
const SAMPLES={
  one:"@@カノン\nヲ～ッホッホッホッホ！\nカノン様のお出ましですわよ～♪",
  many:"@@カノン\nO~hohohohoho!\nLady Kanon has arrived♪\n\n@@ミツキ\nSo this one is the real target.\n\n@@ソノ\nThat obnoxious laugh is making my ears hurt...",
  meta:"@@!title Episode 80-9 — The Tolling Fiend\n@@!subtitle The Death Curse of Geo Nivlhel\n@@!summary Kanon's laughter rings the bell.\\nThe countdown begins.\n\n@@カノン\nO~hohohohoho!\nOut of the way, peasants!\n\n@@ミツキ\nTo put it simply...\nKanon is going to die."
};
function pasteFlow(text){
  const f=file(); if(!f){toast("Buka cerita dulu","warn");return;}
  if(!text||!text.trim()){toast("Clipboard kosong","warn");return;}
  if(isCSIF(text))pasteChooser(text);
  else{
    const b=targetBox(); if(!b)return;
    if(f.view.preview)return toast("Preview read-only — keluar dulu (Alt+3)","warn");
    if(denyLocked(b,"paste"))return;
    const ta=$(`.ta[data-ta="${b.uid}"]`); if(!ta)return;
    pushUndo();
    const s=ta.selectionStart,e=ta.selectionEnd;
    const nv=ta.value.slice(0,s)+text+ta.value.slice(e);
    ta.value=nv; commitText(b,nv); paint(ta);
    ta.focus(); const c=s+text.length; ta.setSelectionRange(c,c);
    renderInspector(); sfx("apply"); toast("Teks biasa ditempel di caret","ok");
  }
}
async function readClip(){
  try{
    if(navigator.clipboard&&navigator.clipboard.readText){ const t=await navigator.clipboard.readText(); pasteFlow(t); return; }
  }catch(e){}
  clipFallback();
}
function clipFallback(){
  modal(`<div class="mhead"><span style="font-size:19px">📎</span><div><h3>Paste payload</h3><div class="sub">Browser menolak akses clipboard — tempel manual atau pakai contoh</div></div><span class="spacer"></span><button class="btn sm icon" data-act="modal-close">✕</button></div>
    <div class="mbody">
      <textarea id="clipTa" style="width:100%;min-height:150px;background:var(--ink-850);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:11px;color:var(--text);font:500 12.5px/1.7 var(--ff-mono);padding:11px" placeholder="Tempel di sini (Ctrl+V)…"></textarea>
      <div class="grouphead">Contoh payload untuk menguji alur</div>
      <div style="display:flex;gap:7px;flex-wrap:wrap">
        <button class="btn sm" data-act="clip-sample" data-v="one">1 block</button>
        <button class="btn sm" data-act="clip-sample" data-v="many">3 blocks</button>
        <button class="btn sm" data-act="clip-sample" data-v="meta">with Box 0 metadata → Import Preview</button>
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="mfoot"><span class="spacer"></span><button class="btn" data-act="modal-close">Cancel</button><button class="btn primary" data-act="clip-go">→ Detect &amp; continue</button></div>`,"sm");
  $("#clipTa").focus();
}

/* ---------- events ---------- */
document.addEventListener("click",e=>{
  const scrim=e.target.closest("[data-scrim]");
  if(scrim&&e.target===scrim){closeModal();return;}
  const el=e.target.closest("[data-act]"); if(!el)return;
  const a=el.dataset.act, f=file();
  const stop=()=>{e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();};
  switch(a){
    case "modal-close": stop(); closeModal(); break;
    case "pop-close": stop(); $("#popRoot").innerHTML=""; break;
    case "dock-toggle": stop(); S.ui.dockOpen=!S.ui.dockOpen; render(); sfx("click"); break;
    case "drawer-toggle": stop(); S.ui.drawerOpen=!S.ui.drawerOpen; render(); sfx("click"); break;
    case "dock-tab":{ stop(); const v=el.dataset.v;
      if(S.ui.dockTab===v&&v==="project"){ openFiles(); toast("Tab diklik lagi → membuka file picker (B10)","info"); }
      else { S.ui.dockTab=v; render(); sfx("click"); } break; }
    case "drawer-tab": stop(); S.ui.drawerTab=el.dataset.v; render(); sfx("click"); break;
    case "open-files": stop(); openFiles(); break;
    case "open-folder": stop(); toast("Open Folder memilih direktori lalu membuka semua .csif/.txt di dalamnya (desktop). Prototipe: pilih berkas ganda.","info"); openFiles(); break;
    case "load-sample": stop(); loadSample(); break;
    case "switch-file":{ stop(); if(e.target.closest("[data-act='close-file']"))return;
      S.activeId=el.dataset.id; S.sel.clear(); S.pinned=null; render(); sfx("click");
      toast(`Beralih ke ${file().name} — view, kunci & box aktif dipulihkan per dokumen`,"info"); break; }
    case "close-file": stop(); closeFile(el.dataset.id); break;
    case "cf-discard":{ stop(); const id=$("#modalRoot").dataset.closeId; closeModal();
      const ff=S.files.find(x=>x.id===id); if(ff){ff.dirty=false;} closeFile(id); break; }
    case "cf-save":{ stop(); const id=el.dataset.id; const ff=S.files.find(x=>x.id===id);
      if(ff){ff.dirty=false;} closeModal(); closeFile(id); sfx("apply"); break; }
    case "save": stop(); saveActive(); break;
    case "export": stop(); exportCSIF(); break;
    case "copy-csif": stop(); if(f){writeClip(serializeCSIF(f));sfx("apply");toast("Seluruh cerita disalin sebagai CSIF","ok");} break;
    case "undo": stop(); doUndo(); break;
    case "redo": stop(); doRedo(); break;
    case "sound": stop(); S.ui.sound=!S.ui.sound; if(S.ui.sound)sfx("toggle"); render(); toast(`UI sound ${S.ui.sound?"on":"muted"}`,S.ui.sound?"ok":"info"); break;
    case "theme": stop(); S.ui.theme=S.ui.theme==="violet"?"daylight":"violet"; render(); sfx("toggle"); toast(`Theme: ${S.ui.theme==="violet"?"Midnight Violet":"Misora Purple & White"}`,"info"); break;
    case "toggle-select": stop(); if(f){f.view.select=!f.view.select; if(!f.view.select)S.sel.clear(); render(); sfx("toggle");
      toast(`Select boxes ${f.view.select?"on — checkbox muncul di setiap header":"off — slot tetap dipesan agar sejajar"}`,"info");} break;
    case "toggle-continuous": stop(); if(f){f.view.continuous=!f.view.continuous; render(); sfx("toggle");
      toast(f.view.continuous?"Story view — semua box mengalir":"Dialogue view — satu box per layar","info");} break;
    case "toggle-preview": stop(); if(f){f.view.preview=!f.view.preview; render(); sfx("toggle");
      toast(f.view.preview?"Preview compiled NGUI · READ-ONLY (B1)":"Kembali ke Raw — bisa mengedit","info");} break;
    case "edit-this":{ stop(); const id=el.dataset.id; f.view.preview=false; f.view.cur=id; render(); sfx("toggle");
      const ta=$(`.ta[data-ta="${id}"]`); if(ta){ta.focus();ta.scrollIntoView({block:"center",behavior:"smooth"});} toast("Mode Raw — silakan edit","ok"); break; }
    case "new-box": stop(); newBox(); break;
    case "cut-box": stop(); cutBoxes(); break;
    case "copy-box": stop(); copyBoxes(); break;
    case "paste-box": stop(); readClip(); break;
    case "del-box": stop(); delBoxes(); break;
    case "lock-box": stop(); toggleLock(el.dataset.id); break;
    case "bulk-lock": stop(); bulkLock(el.dataset.v==="1"); break;
    case "sel-clear": stop(); S.sel.clear(); render(); break;
    case "pin-speaker": stop(); pinSpeaker(); break;
    case "prev": stop(); jump(-1); break;
    case "next": stop(); jump(1); break;
    case "wrap": stop(); wrapSel(el.dataset.o,el.dataset.c||""); break;
    case "ruby": stop(); rubyDialog(); break;
    case "rb-apply":{ stop(); const base=$("#rbBase").value, rd=$("#rbRead").value;
      closeModal(); wrapSel(`[rub:${rd}]${base}`, "[/rub]"); break; }
    case "color-pop": stop(); colorPop(el); break;
    case "c-apply":{ stop(); const c1=$("#c1").value,c2=$("#c2").value,on=$("#gOn").checked;
      const tag=on?`[${c1.slice(1).toUpperCase()}-${c2.slice(1).toUpperCase()}]`:`[${c1.slice(1).toUpperCase()}]`;
      $("#swatch").style.background=on?`linear-gradient(180deg,${c1},${c2})`:c1;
      $("#popRoot").innerHTML=""; wrapSel(tag,"[-]"); break; }
    case "gloss":{ stop(); const g=GLOSSARY[+el.dataset.i]; wrapSel(g.o,g.c); break; }
    case "paste-clip": stop(); readClip(); break;
    case "clip-sample": stop(); $("#clipTa").value=SAMPLES[el.dataset.v]; break;
    case "clip-go":{ stop(); const t=$("#clipTa").value; closeModal(); pasteFlow(t); break; }
    case "pc-insert": stop(); insertPayload(); break;
    case "pc-replace":{ stop(); const t=$("#modalRoot").dataset.payload; closeModal(); importPreview(t,false); break; }
    case "pc-text":{ stop(); const t=$("#modalRoot").dataset.payload; closeModal();
      const b=targetBox(); if(!b)break; if(denyLocked(b,"paste"))break;
      const ta=$(`.ta[data-ta="${b.uid}"]`); if(!ta)break; pushUndo();
      const s=ta.selectionStart,en=ta.selectionEnd;
      const nv=ta.value.slice(0,s)+t+ta.value.slice(en); ta.value=nv; commitText(b,nv); paint(ta); sfx("apply");
      toast("Ditempel sebagai teks biasa (header @@ ikut sebagai teks)","warn"); break; }
    case "ap-apply": stop(); applyImport(); break;
    case "clear-search": stop(); S.ui.query=""; $("#q").value=""; render(); break;
    case "clear-filter": stop(); S.ui.filter="all"; S.ui.query=""; $("#q").value=""; render(); break;
    case "scope": stop(); S.ui.scope=el.dataset.v; render(); break;
    case "filter": stop(); S.ui.filter=el.dataset.v; render(); sfx("click"); break;
    case "jump":{ stop(); const fid=el.dataset.fid,bid=el.dataset.bid;
      S.activeId=fid; const ff=file(); ff.view.cur=bid==="meta"?"meta":bid;
      if(bid==="meta")ff.view.continuous=true;
      render(); sfx("jump");
      const card=$(`[data-card="${bid}"]`); if(card)card.scrollIntoView({block:"center",behavior:"smooth"});
      toast(`Lompat ke ${bid==="meta"?"Box 0 metadata":"Box "+(ff.boxes.findIndex(x=>x.uid===bid)+1)}`,"info"); break; }
    case "gutter": stop(); S.ui.gutter=!S.ui.gutter; render(); sfx("click"); break;
    case "ruler": stop(); S.ui.ruler=!S.ui.ruler; render(); sfx("click"); break;
    case "font": stop(); S.ui.font=clamp(S.ui.font+ +el.dataset.v,12,22); render(); break;
    case "reset-ui": stop(); Object.assign(S.ui,{font:15,gutter:true,ruler:false,tip:"normal",density:"comfortable"}); render(); sfx("toggle"); toast("Preferensi tampilan direset (dokumen tidak disentuh)","ok"); break;
    case "raw-toggle": stop(); S.ui.rawOpen=!S.ui.rawOpen; render(); break;
    case "sv-save":{ stop();
      modal(`<div class="mhead"><h3>Save current view</h3><span class="spacer"></span><button class="btn sm icon" data-act="modal-close">✕</button></div>
        <div class="mbody"><input class="mini" id="svName" style="width:100%;height:34px" placeholder="mis. Proofread Kanon" value="">
        <p style="font:500 11.5px/1.6 var(--ff-ui);color:var(--muted);margin:10px 0 0">Disimpan: continuous ${f?f.view.continuous:"—"} · preview ${f?f.view.preview:"—"} · filter ${S.ui.filter} · sort ${S.ui.sort} · kueri “${esc(S.ui.query)}”.</p></div>
        <div class="mfoot"><span class="spacer"></span><button class="btn" data-act="modal-close">Cancel</button><button class="btn primary" data-act="sv-do">☆ Save</button></div>`,"sm");
      $("#svName").focus(); break; }
    case "sv-do":{ stop(); const nm=$("#svName").value.trim()||("View "+(S.views.length+1));
      S.views.push({name:nm,cont:f?f.view.continuous:false,prev:f?f.view.preview:false,filter:S.ui.filter,sort:S.ui.sort,q:S.ui.query});
      closeModal(); render(); sfx("apply"); toast(`View “${nm}” disimpan`,"ok"); break; }
    case "sv-apply":{ stop(); const v=S.views[+el.dataset.i]; if(!v||!f)break;
      f.view.continuous=v.cont; f.view.preview=v.prev; S.ui.filter=v.filter; S.ui.sort=v.sort; S.ui.query=v.q; $("#q").value=v.q;
      render(); sfx("toggle"); toast(`View “${v.name}” diterapkan`,"ok"); break; }
    case "sv-del": stop(); S.views.splice(+el.dataset.i,1); render(); toast("Saved view dihapus","info"); break;
  }
});
/* inputs */
document.addEventListener("input",e=>{
  const t=e.target;
  if(t.id==="q"){ S.ui.query=t.value; if(t.value.trim())S.ui.dockTab="search"; render(); return; }
  if(t.classList.contains("ta")){
    const f=file(), b=box(f,t.dataset.ta); if(!b)return;
    commitText(b,t.value); paint(t); renderInspector(); return;
  }
  if(t.dataset.spk){ const f=file(), b=box(f,t.dataset.spk); if(!b)return;
    if(b.locked){t.value=b.speaker;denyLocked(b,"mengganti speaker");return;}
    pushUndo(); b.speaker=t.value; b.edited=true; touch(f);
    const card=$(`[data-card="${b.uid}"]`), sp=card?$(".spk",card):null;
    if(sp){sp.textContent=b.speaker||"(narration)";sp.classList.toggle("narr",!b.speaker);}
    renderRaw(); renderStatus(); renderSearch(); return; }
  if(t.dataset.meta){ const f=file(); if(!f)return; pushUndo(); f.meta[t.dataset.meta]=t.value; touch(f); renderRaw(); renderSearch(); return; }
});
document.addEventListener("change",e=>{
  const t=e.target;
  if(t.id==="sort"){S.ui.sort=t.value;render();sfx("click");return;}
  if(t.id==="tipSpeed"){S.ui.tip=t.value;persist();toast(`Tooltip: ${t.value}`,"info");return;}
  if(t.id==="density"){S.ui.density=t.value;render();return;}
  if(t.id==="sizeSel"){ const v=t.value; t.value=""; if(v)wrapSel(`<size=${v}>`,"</size>"); return; }
  if(t.dataset.sel){ const id=t.dataset.sel; if(t.checked)S.sel.add(id);else S.sel.delete(id); render(); sfx("click"); return; }
  if(t.id==="fileInput"){ handleFiles(t.files); t.value=""; return; }
});
/* focus tracking */
document.addEventListener("focusin",e=>{
  const t=e.target;
  if(t.classList&&t.classList.contains("ta")){ const f=file(); if(f&&f.view.cur!==t.dataset.ta){ f.view.cur=t.dataset.ta;
    $$(".card").forEach(c=>c.classList.toggle("active",c.dataset.card===t.dataset.ta));
    renderRaw(); renderInspector(); renderStatus(); } }
  if(t.dataset&&t.dataset.meta){ const f=file(); if(f)f.view.cur="meta"; }
});
/* locked / preview keystroke interception (B1, B9) */
document.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{
  const t=e.target;
  if(t.classList&&t.classList.contains("ta")){
    const f=file(), b=box(f,t.dataset.ta);
    if(b&&b.locked){
      const k=e.key;
      const editing=k.length===1||["Backspace","Delete","Enter","Tab"].includes(k);
      if(editing&&!e.ctrlKey&&!e.metaKey&&!e.altKey){ e.preventDefault(); denyLocked(b,"mengedit"); return; }
      if((e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey)&&["v","x","z","y"].includes(k.toLowerCase())){ e.preventDefault(); denyLocked(b,"mengedit"); return; }
    }
    if(e.key==="Enter"||e.key==="Backspace"||e.key==="Delete"){ requestAnimationFrame(()=>{paint(t);}); }
    return;
  }
  if(t.classList&&t.classList.contains("prev")){
    const k=e.key;
    if((k.length===1||["Backspace","Delete","Enter"].includes(k))&&!e.ctrlKey&&!e.metaKey&&!e.altKey){
      e.preventDefault(); const f=file();
      t.classList.add("nudge"); setTimeout(()=>t.classList.remove("nudge"),360); sfx("err");
      toast("Preview read-only — teks boleh disorot & disalin, tetapi tidak bisa diubah.","warn",
        {label:"✎ Exit preview",fn:()=>{f.view.preview=false;render();const b=curBox();const ta=b?$(`.ta[data-ta="${b.uid}"]`):null;if(ta)ta.focus();}});
    }
    return;
  }
  if(t.dataset&&t.dataset.spk){ const f=file(),b=box(f,t.dataset.spk);
    if(b&&b.locked&&(e.key.length===1||["Backspace","Delete"].includes(e.key))){e.preventDefault();denyLocked(b,"mengganti speaker");} return; }
},true);
/* global shortcuts */
document.addEventListener("keydown",e=>{
  const mod=e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey, k=e.key.toLowerCase();
  const typing=/^(INPUT|TEXTAREA|SELECT)$/.test(e.target.tagName);
  if(e.altKey&&["1","2","3"].includes(e.key)){ e.preventDefault();
    const f=file(); if(!f)return;
    if(e.key==="1"){f.view.select=!f.view.select;if(!f.view.select)S.sel.clear();}
    if(e.key==="2")f.view.continuous=!f.view.continuous;
    if(e.key==="3")f.view.preview=!f.view.preview;
    render(); sfx("toggle"); return; }
  if(e.altKey&&e.key==="ArrowLeft"){e.preventDefault();jump(-1);return;}
  if(e.altKey&&e.key==="ArrowRight"){e.preventDefault();jump(1);return;}
  if(e.altKey&&k==="d"){e.preventDefault();S.ui.dockOpen=!S.ui.dockOpen;render();return;}
  if(e.altKey&&k==="r"){e.preventDefault();S.ui.drawerOpen=!S.ui.drawerOpen;render();return;}
  if(mod&&k==="s"){e.preventDefault();saveActive();return;}
  if(mod&&k==="o"){e.preventDefault();openFiles();return;}
  if(mod&&k==="f"){e.preventDefault();$("#q").focus();$("#q").select();return;}
  if(mod&&k==="n"&&!e.shiftKey){e.preventDefault();newBox();return;}
  if(mod&&k==="l"){e.preventDefault();toggleLock();return;}
  if(mod&&k==="r"){e.preventDefault();rubyDialog();return;}
  if(mod&&e.shiftKey&&k==="c"){e.preventDefault();const f=file();if(f){writeClip(serializeCSIF(f));toast("CSIF disalin","ok");}return;}
  if(mod&&e.shiftKey&&k==="e"){e.preventDefault();exportCSIF();return;}
  if(mod&&e.altKey&&k==="x"){e.preventDefault();cutBoxes();return;}
  if(mod&&e.altKey&&k==="c"){e.preventDefault();copyBoxes();return;}
  if(mod&&e.altKey&&k==="v"){e.preventDefault();readClip();return;}
  if(mod&&k==="z"&&!e.shiftKey){ if(typing&&e.target.classList.contains("ta")){ const f=file(),b=box(f,e.target.dataset.ta); if(b&&b.locked)return; }
    e.preventDefault();doUndo();return;}
  if(mod&&(k==="y"||(e.shiftKey&&k==="z"))){e.preventDefault();doRedo();return;}
  if(mod&&["b","i","u"].includes(k)&&!e.shiftKey){
    if(e.target.classList.contains("ta")){e.preventDefault();wrapSel(`[${k}]`,`[/${k}]`);} return;}
  if(e.key==="Escape"){ if($("#modalRoot").innerHTML){closeModal();} else if(S.ui.query){S.ui.query="";$("#q").value="";render();} return; }
});
/* real Ctrl+V into editors → CSIF-aware */
document.addEventListener("paste",e=>{
  const t=e.target;
  if(!(t.classList&&t.classList.contains("ta")))return;
  const txt=(e.clipboardData||window.clipboardData)?.getData("text")||"";
  const f=file(), b=box(f,t.dataset.ta);
  if(b&&b.locked){ e.preventDefault(); denyLocked(b,"paste"); return; }
  if(isCSIF(txt)){ e.preventDefault(); pasteFlow(txt); return; }
  requestAnimationFrame(()=>{ const bb=box(file(),t.dataset.ta); if(bb){commitText(bb,t.value);paint(t);renderInspector();} });
});
/* scroll sync */
document.addEventListener("scroll",e=>{
  const t=e.target;
  if(t.classList&&t.classList.contains("ta")){
    const w=t.closest(".edwrap"), st=t.closest(".edstack");
    if(w)$(".hl",w).scrollTop=t.scrollTop;
    if(st&&$(".gutter",st))$(".gutter",st).scrollTop=t.scrollTop;
  }
},true);
document.addEventListener("selectionchange",()=>{
  const t=document.activeElement;
  if(t&&t.classList&&t.classList.contains("ta"))paint(t);
});
/* drag & drop reorder */
document.addEventListener("dragstart",e=>{
  const h=e.target.closest?.("[data-drag]"); if(!h||h.getAttribute("draggable")!=="true")return;
  S.dragUid=h.dataset.drag; e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed="move";
  try{e.dataTransfer.setData("text/plain",S.dragUid);}catch(err){}
});
document.addEventListener("dragover",e=>{
  const c=e.target.closest?.("[data-card]");
  if(S.dragUid&&c&&c.dataset.card!==S.dragUid){ e.preventDefault(); c.classList.add("dragover"); }
  const dz=e.target.closest?.("#drop"); if(dz){e.preventDefault();dz.classList.add("hot");}
});
document.addEventListener("dragleave",e=>{
  const c=e.target.closest?.("[data-card]"); if(c)c.classList.remove("dragover");
  const dz=e.target.closest?.("#drop"); if(dz)dz.classList.remove("hot");
});
document.addEventListener("drop",e=>{
  const dz=e.target.closest?.("#drop");
  if(dz){ e.preventDefault(); dz.classList.remove("hot"); if(e.dataTransfer?.files?.length)handleFiles(e.dataTransfer.files); return; }
  const c=e.target.closest?.("[data-card]");
  if(S.dragUid&&c){
    e.preventDefault(); c.classList.remove("dragover");
    const f=file(), from=f.boxes.findIndex(b=>b.uid===S.dragUid), to=f.boxes.findIndex(b=>b.uid===c.dataset.card);
    S.dragUid=null;
    if(from<0||to<0||from===to)return;
    pushUndo(); const [m]=f.boxes.splice(from,1); f.boxes.splice(to,0,m); touch(f); render(); sfx("apply");
    toast(`Box dipindah: #${from+1} → #${to+1}`,"ok",{label:"Undo",fn:doUndo});
    return;
  }
  if(e.dataTransfer?.files?.length){ e.preventDefault(); handleFiles(e.dataTransfer.files); }
});
document.addEventListener("dragend",()=>{S.dragUid=null;$$(".dragover").forEach(c=>c.classList.remove("dragover"));});
/* resize repaint */
let rz=null;
addEventListener("resize",()=>{ clearTimeout(rz); rz=setTimeout(()=>{$$(".ta").forEach(paint);},120); });
if(window.ResizeObserver){ const ro=new ResizeObserver(()=>{$$(".ta").forEach(paint);}); ro.observe($("#edarea")); }

/* ---------- boot ---------- */
restore();
if(innerWidth>1080)S.ui.drawerOpen=true;
S.files=[seedStory()]; S.activeId=S.files[0].id; S.files[0].view.cur=S.files[0].boxes[0].uid;
render();
requestAnimationFrame(()=>{$$(".ta").forEach(paint);});
setTimeout(()=>toast("Prototipe keputusan B1–B10 siap. Buka tab <b>📝 Keputusan</b> di drawer kanan untuk vonis tiap temuan.","info"),700);
setTimeout(()=>toast("Coba: <b>Alt+3</b> preview lalu ketik (B1) · klik 🔒 lalu ketik (B9) · 📌 lalu Next (pin) · <b>📎 Paste</b> untuk alur CSIF.","info"),2100);
</script>
</body>
</html>
```
